    Chapter 1

    Another day in the labs, hunched over a microscope. It was her life for the last 3 years since she became the head of R&D of Luthor Corp. It used to be her dream job, with endless possibilities and all dreamy goals on curing cancer or advancing the AI. The reality was harsh, bitter and Lena felt crushed with every passing day she spent in her family’s company. Which will be her legacy someday. Hopefully, never she thought.


    Her assistant Jess came in a hurry, her cheeks all red.

    “Miss Luthor, he is expecting you in his office.” Lena sighed. Looking at Jess, it was going to be either scolding Lena in front of self-privileged men or boosting his ego by belittling Lena’s work. The latter was Lex’s popular choice lately.

    “Okay Jess, thank you.” She finished up her work and went to her older brother’s office, dragging her feet. It was uniquely quiet, normally Lex or Lillian would get off from the attention of their minions. Ominous she thought. She knocked and went inside. The room was dark and Lex was sitting behind his desk, his back to Lena.


    “I heard you wanted to see me?” She was impatient, she had many things to do in her lab so she would rather get on with whatever it was.

    “I don’t have time to play our usual games Lex, so tell me what’s that you wanted to talk about.” He turned to face Lena, and she got shivers on her back by the evil smirk on his face.

    “You were always little rebellious omega, weren’t you dear sister?” Were? She kept her icy façade.

    “Since you reminded me of my place in this damned world I’m returning to my work Lex, have a good evening.” She turned to leave the office but Lex’s sinister laugh stopped her.

    “Okay Lena, I don’t have much time so I’ll be brief.” He got up and went to face Lena directly. He placed his hands on Lena’s shoulders, grasping her firmly. He had a malicious glint in his eyes, Lena felt frozen with fear of what’s coming next.

    “After all those years now you have the chance to become very powerful my dear sister, now finally you can be a use to me, to our family.” Lena tried to not tremble under his hands.

    “You know how much I despise those creatures-those aliens who think they are gods among us. I was trying to get rid of them, trying to save humanity but then I thought was there an easier way? Do you remember the story of the fall of Troy? Greeks used a wooden horse to deceive Trojans, the infamous Trojan horse. Now you will be my little Trojan horse for the Kryptonian pests.” Lena was breathing heavily, her mind was failing to catch up with what Lex was saying.

    “What do you mean Lex?” Her voice shook and Lex smiled widely in front of her sister’s distraught figure.

    “I have arranged a marriage between you and crowned princess of Krypton, Kara Zor-El dear sister. You will be her mate and you will bear her pups then you will help me destroy them from inside.” Lena felt her legs giving away, she was trying very hard not to collapse. Marriage? Mating? With a Kryptonian? What the fuck was happening?

    Lex let go of her arms and Lena stumbled, trying to find a surface to lean on. Yes, Lex was cruel, evil, and merciless but surely he wouldn’t?

    “I don’t know what the hell you planned Lex but if you think I’m gonna do whatever you say you are crazier than everyone thinks.” She gathered her strength and tried to leave his office but two burly men were blocking her path. She tried to bypass them but they easily held her not letting her go.

    “You see, I’m not a sloppy planner Lena. I knew you would react like this so I have a little thing for you.” He opened the TV on the wall, the news was on and they were reporting a bombing in one of the Luthor Corp hospitals. Lena gasped and yanked herself. Due to an unknown reason, one of the children’s hospital was blown off and there were many casualties and injured. Lex opened a map where all the Luthor Corp hospitals were marked with blinking red light, and he was holding a little remote. He toyed with it and pressed a button, back in the news there was another reported bombing but details were yet to be announced.


    Lena felt her heart-stopping, it was one of the purest things she ever accomplished or she would like to think so; she had built many hospitals for children across America equipped with the latest technology. Her creation. Her hospitals. Her responsibility. It was the most important thing she took pride in, her dream project and now it was all in danger. Because Lex wanted her to marry a Kryptonian. Or he wanted her to destroy Kryptonians. As if she could. As if it were that simple.


    Kryptonians were the strongest alien species co-habiting in the world and they were proved to be invincible. Almost. Lex had always a grudge against them, especially against one of their declared royals-Kal-El or Superman as humans would call him. He was the first Kryptonian that set foot on earth, he had many god-like qualities different than other Kryptonians who were not as powerful as him. Rumors were saying that he was so powerful because he was from the House of El. One of the last of his house, his lineage. He made his debut by saving the earth from other hostile species which were trying to invade the world and enslave humans. There was utter chaos back then when aliens were living with humans under hidden identities without revealing themselves. And with this attack some of them had to reveal themselves to help the humans, Kal-El was one of them.


    Lex was furious when this Superman as humans called him took his chance of becoming a hero from him, ever since he was trying to harm him or to find any way to make Superman disappear. A couple of years back, an alien ship full of Kryptonians landed on earth making Lex’s plans falter, he had more than a single Kryptonian to get rid of. And apparently, besides Kal-El, there was another important royal blood of Krypton who lived among humans secretly; Kara Zor-El or lately known as Supergirl. She was an enigma to most people, only appearing when there was a catastrophe not in the spotlight like her cousin and her civilian identity still unknown. But Lena was sure that Lex already knew her earth identity, keeping it secret as leverage to use it later.


    With all the dark thoughts swirling inside her head, Lena was desperate. She could kill Lex. She had planned it before, many times. Especially in her mind, she was killing him every day but in reality, her plans were nearly impossible. Her brother was a paranoid genius. With an uncooked attempt, Lena would be dead. Maybe being dead was not so horrible compared to her current situation. She watched the news with tears in her eyes. She promised herself to be strong no matter what happens. She could get through this if only she had more time to cultivate a plan. But there was no time. The only time was the present as she had a choice to make.


    “As much as I want to spend more time with you Lena, I need your answer right now. Or should I blow up a couple of more of your little projects to convince you?” He sneered, and Lena tried not to punch him in the face. He was trying to provoke her on purpose to see how far he could push Lena’s buttons. Lena pushed all the dark thoughts swirling in her head and tried to test Lex, he wouldn’t plan something less than perfect but still.

    “What makes you think she-Supergirl would agree to this? I’m sure she’s not naïve as you think she is.” Lex smiled and rubbed his hands. When he was cheerful like this he was looking like a bald Chuckie. Goddamn him.

    “Oh my dear sister, don’t worry about her. She is very much into this agreement, she even came up with the offer. I guess you have a little Luthor charm, eh?” It was fucking unbelievable.

    Lena didn’t know Supergirl, she never talked with her, hell she was sure they never saw each other apart from news obviously. Why would Supergirl want to marry her, to mate with her? What was going on?

    “As much as I want to chit chat with you for ole time’s sake Lena, I need to know we are in the same page.” Lena responded with a unbridled venom in her voice.

    “What page are we talking about? What exactly you want me to do? To let a Kryptonian knot me, breed me?” She spat out. The thought of being knotted by the most powerful being in the earth gave her goosebumps.

    “Yes Lena, I want her to knot you to breed you and to give you pups. That’s the exact order of things I want from you.” He said with a feigned patience.

    “Fuck you Lex.”

    “I thought something more classical too you know, I know how much you care about some people apart from your little projects. So if this hospital thing is not enough for to convince you, consider their well-being. You know, I know how to motivate people.” Lena felt her heart stopping, he wouldn’t would he? He was probably bluffing, surely he didn’t know. Lena was always discreet and careful whom she talked and met to avoid situations like this. Lex looked at the shaken state of Lena and probed further.

    “Oh yes-I know all about your so-called friendship with Samantha Arias. If I recall correctly, she was working for us, hmm? Well I’m not sure about the extent of your relationship, you seemed pretty cozy; perhaps you let your beta friend fuck you occasionally? Oh also let’s not forget her daughter, she had a daughter am I right, Lena? What was her name, Ruby?” It was a final straw for Lena.

    “Don’t you dare you bastard.”

    “I don’t need to remind who is the bastard here dear sister.” Lena had endured all the insults about her being the bastard Luthor and all that torment but he threating Sam and Ruby was the final push. They were the only thing resembling to a family for Lena.


    She smashed a nearby tumbler and without caring about the cut on her hand she pushed the broken glass to Lex’s carotid artery. Then two men snatched her up, making her double over glass already discarded. Lex’s shrill laugh was resonating in her ears when a whoosh sound brought a familiar figure to the room.


    There stood Supergirl, in her dark red cape looking like an avenging angel.

    “Get your hands off of her.”

    Chapter 2

    “Who is our last patient Nia?”

    Kara was changing her gloves for the last patient of the day, she couldn’t wait for this day to be over. Not that she could get tired with physical labor but there were a lot of things to unpack. Too much drama.

    “That would be this handsome Kara!” Nia brought a big, cheery, black labrador whose name was Buddy. Kara immediately found herself cooing to the doggie in front of her, all the chaos in her mind forgotten.

    She loved her job, more likely she adored her job-her own shabby clinic, flexible work hours, interacting with animals- it was the best job she could ask for. That’s why she chose to do it instead of being a carbon copy of her cousin. Ugh her cousin. Such a prick.

    He was partially one to blame for her current status, she was forced to do something or more accurately she had to force someone to do something.

    After her latest patient Kara was just cleaning up her office, ready to go upstairs to take a shower Alex came knocking down doors. She sighed internally.

    “What the fuck Kara? What the hell were you thinking?” She was half shouting, with her super hearing Kara could felt other alpha’s voice ringing in her brain. She gritted her teeth.

    “Alex you remember what we have talked about screaming-“ Alex came close, invading her personal space, if Kara didn’t have a solid restraint on her alpha she could growl and feel challenged. But this was Alex and she got used to the aggressiveness of the older alpha.

You’d show me your wedding band and ‘hey Alex what’s up and you know I just got married!’ , is that it? Also with a fucking Luthor, seriously Kara? What the hell were you thinking?” Her face was red with all the anger and the lashing out, she looked like seconds away from kicking Kara’s ass.

    “Just calm down Alex, it’s not like I was planning anything, and also Clark shouldn’t have told you anything, I was planning to tell you by myself.” Alex folded her arms and prompted her to continue.

Winn asked what he was talking about, he told about the deal between Rhea and Lex Luthor; apparently in exchange for a trade deal between Luthor Corp and Daxam Lex gave his sister to him. Like he literally sold her. And Mon-El said it was about trade but I didn’t believe him, considering his background probably Lex Luthor’s trying to buy alien mercenary from Daxam.” Kara said in a breath as she was trying to cut this short, she had to clean up herself then she had to go to see Lena. Her wife-to-be. Ugh.

I asked Kal-El to help me to stop Lex but he didn’t believe that Lex is trying something big, he’s busy playing house with his wife so he doesn’t want to get his hands dirty. Lex was determined to marry off her sister, I had to step in so I went to Lex Luthor and proposed to marry her sister.” She said exasperatedly. Alex looked at Kara suspiciously and narrowed her eyes.

Lena always hated flying and now she was flying off to god knows where in the arms of a Kryptonian. But those were really strong arms. Muscled arms. Toned. So fit. Ugh. There was a sofa in the middle and a couple of fluffy-looking blankets were draped on it, there was an easel in the corner in front of a big window. She looked around and approached Supergirl, she had her back to Lena but she was anxiously pacing like she wanted to wear a hole on the ground. As Lena came to stand next to her she offered a tight smile.

She mumbled an okay and again Supergirl was gone and back in a flash carrying a medical kit. She gestured for Lena to sit on one of the stools in the open kitchen and sat next to her.

Lena couldn’t help but wonder about the woman in front of her. Who was she? What was she doing as a civilian if she did have a normal life? But there were more pressing matters to discuss first. Like their marriage.

    Chapter 3

    Chapter Notes

    Lena Luthor is sassy. And she rocks.

    Thank you for the comments and kudos, you guys are awesome!

    Also a side note, I suggest you to listen Human being by Robyn while ur reading


    Lena had a boring 25 years of life filled with work, work and work, and nothing else. Well, not entirely anything else.


    She had the dreadful part of her life in Luthor mansion after Luthors adopted her, then she went to study in MIT where she got multiple Master’s and Ph.D. degrees in a short span of time, she had enough flings with alphas, omegas, and betas but nothing serious. She never had a friendship that lasted more than six months till she met Sam Arias. She never had any pets. She didn’t have hobbies outside work because ‘they are for normal people Lena, you are a Luthor you have to be better than everyone’ as Lex once had said.


    Once she had a normal relationship with Lex. Not so normal but still, something resembling normalcy. They used to play chess together, Lex would teach her how to solve complex calculus problems or he would talk about quantum mechanics for hours and Lena would be enchanted.


    She loved her brother once. And mistakenly she thought her brother loved her back. But Lex was not capable of loving someone or something as Lena found out not so long after. Since then she didn’t try to challenge her brother, she tried to keep a low profile, not to attract his attention because she knew how he loved to torment her both physically and mentally.


    So she had spent most of her 25 years keeping her head down, not really participating in Lex’s evil schemes but also not openly opposing them. She didn’t expect him to acknowledge her value or appreciate her work, overall she didn’t expect anything good from him but she also didn’t expect to be slaved over like this. A piece of omega meat, something to be traded nothing more.


    She was trying to wrap her head around what Supergirl was saying in the last 20 minutes but her brain was short-circuiting. It was already too much for one day. Only one fucking day.


    “Long story short, my brother was auctioning me as a prize and you volunteered, did I get it right?” She cut off the babbling woman, the more she listened to the Girl of Steel the faster her anger subsided but there was still annoyance and disbelief. At least Supergirl looked bothered enough to ensure that she didn’t have premeditated plans to request ownership of her.

    “Yes-I mean not that I volunteered-I did but obviously because I had to, you see I have told you Lex would have sent you off to marry Mon-El otherwise and Rhea-”

    Lena rubbed her temples and sighed loudly. Supergirl shut her mouth immediately, offered an understanding look. She was still too timid around Lena.

    “You are probably tired, do you want to lie down? Oh-I’m sorry I never asked are you hungry? I can get you anything, I’m also always hungry we can-”

    Lena cut her off again. They had skipped some basic introductions.

    “I never asked, where is this place, where are we?” Supergirl offered a small smile and looked bashful in her glorious super suit, what a juxtaposition Lena thought.

    “Well, it’s my house. And I have a clinic downstairs.” She said while fiddling with her cape. Lena noted this was an anxious gesture.

    “Clinic? Are you a doctor?” She asked, she didn’t know whether Supergirl would be okay to share her identity.

    “Yes-no, well I’m a veterinarian actually.” She offered. Then she looked like she forgot to say something very important.

    “Golly! I’m sorry I never introduced myself properly, my name is Kara, Kara Danvers. Actually, it is Kara Zor-El but Danvers is the last name of the family that adopted me when I came to earth.”

    Lena was shocked. She disclosed her human identity without stalling like it was a natural thing to do. Considering she was a Luthor, Lena expected her to be more wary but also they were to be wed so maybe it was normal? Also did she say Golly?


    Supergirl-Kara saw the shocked expression on Lena’s face, her identity was a well-kept secret especially from a Luthor. But considering the circumstances, Kara knew she had to tell Lena everything, not hide things from her. Well, at least she shouldn’t hide much. She took a long breath and tried to be concise as possible.


    “I was sent to earth by my parents to look after my baby cousin, Kal-El or Superman as you probably know him. Our planet was on the brink of destruction, so my parents decided to send me here and during an explosion, my pod drifted away from the earth’s course to somewhere called Phantom Zone. I spent many years there, stuck.” She took a deep breath. It was never an easy thing to talk about.


    Lena could saw the pain in those azure eyes, she didn’t know the woman in front of her apart from her name and her reputation, but she longed to console her. To tangle her hands through blond locks, to tell her that it was okay now. It was a stupid thing to think really, considering they were practically strangers.

    “So-when I landed on earth, my cousin had already grown up he had just come out as a hero. I was just thirteen, on a different planet with the people I didn’t know anything about. All alone. My cousin was busy with being a superhero and with his life, so he left me with Danvers. They took care of me, taught me the earth customs and I became a part of their family. I was living my life as a normal person, till my cousin came to me to ask help to defeat some rogue aliens. Since then they created this persona, Supergirl so that I could keep a part of my life normal.”

    Lena was trying to absorb as much information as she can, but she could feel the exhaustion of the day looming over her.

    “Your cousin looks like a prick.” Lena didn’t hold back, Superman was a beloved hero on earth but for whatever reason, she never liked him no matter how peaceful he looked in the news. Kara chuckled at Lena’s remark.

    “Something we both agree.” She admitted slowly, it was a sore subject obviously as Lena observed her gloomy expression.

    “You said they created Supergirl, who are they?” She probed but Supergirl shook her head.

    Alex was gonna be furious once she learned that Kara had disclosed her identity to Lena Luthor, so she didn’t want to piss her off more by telling all that DEO stuff.

    “It-it’s nothing, Lena you look very tired why don’t you lie down?” She tried again, she knew Lena didn’t trust her yet but Kara was thinking about her well-being. The physical and mental toll of what happened could be easily seen in Lena’s state.

    “We still have things to discuss but I don’t think I can continue more today. Can you call me a cab so I can go to my place?” Lena asked while she rose from her seat. She didn’t have a chance to bring her bag, her purse, or her coat as Supergirl had literally dragged her here.

Now thanks to you, all my things are left in Luthor Corp.” She scolded Girl of Steel. Kara also stood up, coming next to Lena she didn’t know what to do with her hands, Lena looked like she was about to collapse to the ground and she wanted to support her but also she didn’t want to make her angry.

    “I-I-I’m sorry, I just didn’t want to talk in Luthor Corp and you looked so angry and I just did-”

    It was so bizarre to see Supergirl stuttering in front of her, looking apologetic which was very different Lena had seen of her from the news. She looked always so confident, bordering on arrogant and self-righteous; it was kind of refreshing to see her like this.

    “Cab?” She cut in her babbling.

    “Oh I can drop you off wherever you want, there is no need for a cab.” The mere mention of flying strapped to a body made her feel queasy.

    “No, I want to go by myself. So, my cab?”



    The next morning Lena woke up with a headache. Also, her hand still throbbed with pain, the effects of the painkillers she took before going to bed wore off. She mopped herself from the bed and started to get ready for the day.


    It was the normal thing to do, get up early and get ready for work. Her life was not normal anymore and she didn’t want to return to Luthor Corp or to see the disgusting face of her brother but she had unfinished projects that she couldn’t abandon.


    She and Supergirl-or Kara had agreed to talk tonight in her penthouse, in detail about the things they couldn’t talk about last night. Ugh. The marriage thing.

    The topic was dreadful. Lena never imagined herself getting hitched but now they were to be married. With Supergirl... Surely it was not that simple, Lex arranged for her to marry her off and that’s it? She was not a meek, compliant little omega and she didn’t have to do anything Lex wanted. She had a plan set in her mind.


    The only problem was Lex’s. He was the only variable in the equation, unstable and unpredictable. He had bluntly threatened to hurt Sam or Ruby so she had to make sure they were safe and they would be safe. The other problems with hospitals, that she could take care of. At least she hoped so.


    Jess greeted her and although she was obviously worried and curious about what had happened yesterday-with her boss’ hand bandaged and all- she didn’t ask anything; it was a blessing as Lena was not in the mood of talking. She wanted to get on with the work she had left.


    By 7 pm promptly, she left the work since she had to meet with Supergirl; she dismissed her driver and left with her car. Maybe driving could help her to clear her mind.


    But of course, she didn’t think about the traffic in National City; she was stuck in traffic minutes later. She cursed and accepted her fate, she was gonna be there for a while. She was so lost in her thoughts, as the traffic jam dissolved she revved the engine and took off, not noticing the truck coming onto her at full speed.

    Just when she noticed the truck, she hit the brakes suddenly, tires screeched loudly but it was too late she was about to get under that damned truck.

    Only a breath later Supergirl appeared punching her hands on the bonnet of the truck stopping it immediately. There was a commotion outside with the almost accident and with the presence of the city’s beloved hero. Lena was shaken, she was sure she was gonna get crushed under the truck if Supergirl didn’t show up.

    She tried to calm her galloping heart, Supergirl warned the truck’s driver as he was at fault driving through wrong lanes-then she appeared next to Lena’s window in a flash. She opened the door and tried to scan her body to see if she’s okay.

    “Lena are you okay? Are you hurt?” She asked with a genuine concern. Lena managed to speak out. <*p>

    “I’m fine.” Supergirl scanned her a couple of times trying to make sure that she is okay. As they were blocking the traffic, a couple of drivers blasted the horns.

    “Can I take you home or can you go-” Lena took a deep breath and closed the door.

    “I can go by myself.” She took off without thanking her and cursing herself for her stupidity on the way to her penthouse.


    It was a couple of hours later; Lena heard a knock on the balcony door while she was working on the kitchen countertop. She had drank enough wine to sedate a horse. Maybe that was why her heart started to beat like crazy when she saw her. She had practiced her talk, it would take under a minute if she wasn’t interrupted.

    Supergirl-well, not Supergirl, it was Kara in her civilian clothes- waving her sheepishly.

    She went to open the door and let her in. In her jeans and an oversized t-shirt, Supergirl-Kara looked so normal and so different from the Supergirl. Her in Supergirl regalia was dashing but intimidating but her as Kara was cute and handsome. Handsome. Those bulging biceps were something else. Yes, I was clutching to them yesterday. Also she was wearing glasses? She’s wearing glasses?


    “Um-h-hi Lena, are you okay? Are you sure you’re not hurt? You scared me there-”

    “It’s okay Su—Kara, I’m okay, and thank you by the way.” The smile on her face so big, Lena could feel the warmth of her smile; she found her lips curling upwards involuntarily.

    “What?” Lena asked.

    Kara averted her eyes and her cheeks blushed to a faint pink. At that moment Lena decided she liked to see her smile.

    “I- just-it’s the first time you call me Kara that’s all.”

    “Would you prefer if I call you Supergirl?” Lena couldn’t skip the chance of teasing her.

    “No no no-it’s just-I just liked it-oh you are teasing me, aren’t you?” Kara scolded her playfully. Lena couldn’t help but chuckle. This banter was quite a juxtaposition to the gravity of their current status. As her playfulness dimmed, Kara also got serious.

    “So I think we have to talk about this-this marriage.”

    Lena nodded. Be brief Lena she told herself.

    “Yes, although you did try to explain things to me, I still don’t know why you tried to help me with putting yourself forward. I mean we don’t even know each other, we have never met yet you still volunteered to marry a woman you know nothing about.”

    She could see Kara biting her lower lip, trying not to interfere. Lena steeled herself, getting into the role of classic boss-bitch.


    “But all of that, they are not important now. I don’t know what type of arrangement you have with Lex but I’m not something to be traded or to be exchanged. I will not marry you or anyone else I didn’t choose. I don’t give two fucks about what Lex or anyone wants from me. Do you understand?” She could see Kara flinching with her choice of words but she looked like she was expecting this outburst.

    “Yes Lena I understand, I’m not expecting something from you I just wanted to help you that’s all. I didn’t plan to gain anything from this as I didn’t plan any of this.”

    Kara sighed as she ran her hands through her hair. Lena didn’t recognize her from years ago and Kara didn’t expect her to remember but she wanted Lena to trust her. She needed to be careful with her. She wanted to build solid trust. And maybe a friendship in the future? Kara wouldn’t oppose the idea of something more too…

    “I don’t know what Lex had told you or how he forced you to marry me; I just want you to know that you can tell me if he’s threatening you or he’s planning to do something.”

    Lena snorted, if Lex had put something to his mind, he would do it. No matter what it took and no matter what it costed. Lena knew his brother well enough that Supergirl or Superman couldn’t prevent him if he was determined. Bringing Supergirl into this would only cause her to get hurt, nothing more. And Lena didn’t want Supergirl to be the focus of her evil brother like Superman.


    “Thank you Supergirl for the offer but I can handle my brother.”

    The light in Kara’s eyes dimmed as Lena referred to her Supergirl again. Her shoulders slumped as she inspected the determination in Lena’s eyes. She nodded briefly and turned to the balcony to leave as she came.

    “Just in case, if you ever need me, call me and I will be there.” She said before she went.

    Lena shut the door after she left. She dismissed a little voice inside her head telling her she did wrong to shut down the alpha. They were never gonna get married, it was doomed from the beginning. She couldn’t help but feel a bit dejected. What was wrong with her?

    She did the right thing, was Kara expecting them to marry for real looking like a kicked puppy?


    She could rethink about getting help from Kara later if her plan didn’t work, but first, she had to make sure that Sam and Ruby were safe. She picked up her phone and called Sam.

    “Hey Sam-did you guys arrive there yet?”

    Chapter 4

    Chapter Notes

    So, will Lena come around? A huge bomb on this chapter ppl!

    Kara was busy munching the last batch of the potstickers Alex had brought. Her friends were sprawled all over her living room, they were having an intense discussion on Pictionary. Normally she would join in, she would try to win or whine over losing but tonight her mind was somewhere else.


    It had been two days since the last she spoke with Lena. When she had offered herself to marry Lena and Lex agreed to it, she really didn’t expect to marry her. It was obvious that Lex was trying to force Lena into something she wasn’t even aware of. She just wanted to be an escape for all, that’s it. Just provide some time to sort things out, even help her if she needs she told herself. Despite all of these, Kara couldn’t help but feel a bit disappointed. Apparently, Lena didn’t remember her from years ago so she wasn’t going to bring that up. But Kara remembered her. She remembered her well. Lena was special but she wasn’t aware of her own worth. She was gorgeous and she had a good heart which was rare these days. Anyone who was meant to marry Lena would be a lucky person. Ugh. She didn’t want to think about other people, thinking about her with other people made her insides clench.


    Alex’s voice brought her back.

    “Hey, you have been quiet today.” She was suspiciously looking at her little sister, Kara was always the cheerful one among them. Kara just shrugged and offered a half-hearted smile.

    “I guess I’m tired.”

    Nia chipped in.

    “We were packed today! I can’t believe we have so many customers around this neighborhood. Though I’ve noticed the majority of the dog owners were some pretty omegas y’know, always dropping by, maybe they were looking for a hot date with a hot vet-” Kara felt her cheeks heat up. She was aware of the effect she had on people but talking about it was making her nervous. And also she didn’t have care for any of them.

    “Nia!” She prompted Nia to shut up. Nia cackled and winked at her, returning to help Brainy. Although he was a 12th level intellect he was having a hard time understanding the basics of Pictionary. Poor guy.

    Winn was telling something to James, Kara eavesdropped involuntarily when she heard Lena’s name.

    “Dude, you should have seen him; everyone was mocking with him, he was not happy, and he thrashed the place, eventually someone had to kick him out-”

    James snorted.

    “All because he didn’t get to mate with a Luthor? Poor bastard doesn’t know he got off easily-”

    Kara squeezed the beer bottle in her hand a bit tight, it crushed to dust. She did know that James didn’t like Luthors, he especially didn’t like Lena but the way he talked made her angry. If she didn’t know James or they didn’t have a bit of history, she would have taught him a lesson.

    Good thing that besides Alex no one saw her dusting away what was left of the bottle.

    Alex sidled up to Kara with wine in her hand.

    “Have you heard anything from her?” As always, Alex did see right through her.

    “No but as I said I wasn’t expecting anything-”

    “Cut the bullshit Kara, I don’t know what happened between you two or how do you know each other but it seems like you care about her; I mean you care her enough that you actually offered to marry her-”

    All the attention in the room shifted to them.

    “What?”

    “What marriage you guys talking about?”

    “Kara dude, were you gonna marry and you didn’t tell us?!”

    “What the hell Kara!”



    Lena was hurrying to leave, she was trying to ignore Lex for the past two days; he was calling her, stopping by her office but thanks to Jess she was successful at avoiding him. Till now obviously.

    Lex looked cheerful and relaxed, two things Lena wasn’t anticipating from him. She was expecting him to throw a fit or thrash her office but there was Lex, casually leaning her office door with that evil glint in his eyes. Here we go again she thought.

    “Are you going to watch me all night or…?” She kept stuffing things into her bag, refusing to look at him. Lex sneered and went to sit on her chair. He swirled on the chair, Lena clenched her chin. She could get through it again-

    “Turn on the Super-proof mode, Eve.” Lex called out for his AI. He had installed a crazy AI to the whole Luthor Corp like a megalomaniac he was. Actually, Lena was one of the lead architects of this AI till Lex snatched it away from her. Like he did to most of the things Lena had created.

    “Super-proof mode on Mr. Luthor” Lena didn’t know anything about a ‘Super-proof’ mode. Hell, she didn’t know anything about what Lex was doing anymore.

    “What is this?” She asked.

    “Oh, a little precaution since your betrothed could be listening to us talking.” He kept shuffling on the chair, observing Lena closely.

    “Why are you avoiding me, dear?”

    She stopped and looked at him finally.

    “I’m not.”

    Lex tutted.

    “You are. I was angry at first, we didn’t have the chance to finish our talk and you ghosted me but now we are both here. I presume you had enough time to think everything over. So, when is the happy union?”

    Lena sighed tiredly. She had to reproach him carefully or he could lash out.

    “There won’t be any union, Lex. I’m not yours to give or take.” Lex stopped his swirling and stood up. Lena instinctively took a step back. Here it comes.

    “I don’t think you are aware of the gravity of the situation dear sister. I told you, if you don’t do as I say I’ll make Sam and Ruby Arias-” Lena interrupted him. She already took care of them but Lex didn’t know about it.

    “You can try if you will be able to find them of course.”

    Lex measured her then offered a knowing smile.

    “I expected better from you Lena, really, where is that girl kicking my ass in chess? I know you located them somewhere ‘safe’. Hmm, where was it…Belfast?”

    Lena knew Lex would track her movements, so she had constructed a solid plan. To divert him. She kept her cool and didn’t respond to him.

    “I guess not, hmm? So assuming it was a decoy, you are keeping them somewhere nearby. It’s okay, I can still find them. But that’s not all, I have got many tricks up in my sleeves darling sister. You know, when I heard Belfast I was kind of excited, I thought I could kill two birds with one stone, well in our case it’s three birds.”

    Lex took out a picture from his suit, a photograph; and he put it on the table.

    “Do you remember her Lena?”

    Lena approached table. It was a picture of a woman with grizzled hair, green eyes, and those eyes…Lena recognized those eyes from somewhere. It had a very familiar shade, like…

    “I guess you don’t know her. Such a shame really. It’s been 21 years but wouldn’t a daughter recognize her mother?”

    Lena’s breath hitched and her sight went blurry. Of course. She knew that shade of green from herself. From every time she looked up to the mirror.

    “No-no, you are lying. She’s dead. I know she’s dead. She died-”

    She tried weakly. Lex directed her to sit on one of the chairs.

    “We have much to talk about. So I have your attention now, where were we?”



    Kara was at the DEO, waiting for Alex to finish her work, so they could go home together. They had planned a sisters’ night, but there was a last-minute robbery somewhere downtown. She dawdled with Winn, talking about a new Chinese place opened nearby.

    She wasn’t a permanent figure in DEO, most of the agents were still wary around her as they didn’t trust her completely; she was helping out occasionally, but she was not going to missions regularly. She didn’t want to be tied to some made-up regulations which were forced by those in power. She knew firsthand how those powerful ones loved to deceit their people when the time came. As once her parents did to Krypton. And now Krypton was long gone, a pile of dust in the space.

    So no, here on earth she was meant to make a fresh start. A start where she won’t be the atlas taking up whole the burden of the world. She would help, save the day occasionally – well mostly- but that was it. She wanted people not to expect too much from her. All this arrangement worked for her, she could spend her time however she liked, in the clinic or with her friends. She was happy that way.


    She heard Lena calling for her- she immediately listened to her heartbeat which was beating normal, but she couldn’t help but worry.

    “Winn, can you tell Alex that I’ll meet her in the home, I have a thing to take care of first?”

    She didn’t wait for a reply and quickly took off. She was on top of her penthouse seconds later. She tried to keep her heart steady as she approached Lena. The door to the balcony was left open. She landed with a thud and scanned the place for any danger, there was none. It was only soft-looking Lena, in her hoodie and yoga pants sipping her scotch. Kara had already noticed that Lena loved a strong drink, especially when she was under stress.

    “Lena?”

    Lena looked up to her and Kara’s pulse quickened. When she was near Lena, she was like a moth to a flame; her senses were going overdrive. Other than her usual icy façade she noticed the downcast expression in her beautiful eyes.

    “Hi. Thank you for coming.” Her voice was hoarse and raw like she was crying.

    “Are you okay, did something happen?”

    Kara felt anxious as Lena silently shook her head. She averted her gaze and went to the open kitchen to refill her glass. Judging by the bottle she was drinking for a while.

    “Do you want a drink?” She asked, Kara was about to reply then Lena scoffed.

    “Sorry I forgot, these drinks probably don’t affect you.”

    “Still I’d like a glass.”

    Kara observed her surroundings, the last time she was there it didn’t last more than 5 minutes as Lena had sent her away. It was certainly a rich-looking apartment, but devoid of colors, everything looked so sterile, untouched. Like a luxurious hotel room. Lena came with a half-full glass and flopped down on the couch, gesturing her to sit as well.

    Kara tried to maintain a respectable distance between them, and she fiddled with the glass in her hand. Lena looked lost in her thoughts so she tried again.

    “Lena, did something happen?”

    Lena blinked up twice and met with her eyes, her expression blank.

    “Does your offer still stand?” Kara scrunched up her brows.

    “Which offer are you talking about?”

    Lena took a deep breath before continuing.

    “About marrying me.”

    Kara was surprised, she wasn’t expecting this. She tried to gauge her face but Lena was looking at her coolly. She knew something was wrong.

    “What happened? What did Lex do?”

    She inched closer to Lena. Lena’s face was still devoid of emotions.

    “Nothing has happened. I-I just..” She sighed and got up from the couch, turning her back to Kara.

    “I can see something’s bothering you Lena and last time we have talked, you made it clear you didn’t have any intention of marrying me. So what has changed?” She also stood up, crossed her arms. Lena clenched her fists and sighed again.

    “I told you, there is nothing-”

    “If you don’t want to talk here, I can get us to somewhere safe so you can-”

    “You are not listening! I told you there is nothing wrong!” Lena exploded, her bottom lip slightly trembling.

    Kara shut her mouth quickly, taking in the angry goddess in front of her. She was beautiful, yes, and she was extra beautiful when she was angry. But she was not gonna bring that up as the goddess in front of her could murder her with a single glance.

    Obviously there was something seriously wrong that was making Lena act this way.

    “Lena, I don’t know what happened or what Lex had done to force you, but I promise you if you tell me, I can…” She tried again but Lena was not listening.

    “I said do you still want me? Yes or no?”

    Her emerald eyes were burning with a wave of unadulterated anger.

    Kara knew if Lena could trust her to tell everything, she would do anything in her power to make things right.

    “Lena, I…”

    Lena folded her arms and raised one of her perfectly manicured eyebrows.

    “I-I’m sorry to bring this up on you, but I have to have an answer now. I-Otherwise I’m to marry with Mon-El of Daxam.” She said defeatedly.

    Kara felt her anger simmering in the mentioning of Mon-El, she couldn’t stand that piece of shit; she would rather tear his spleen before he got near to Lena.

    “No, I told you Mon-El is not a nice person-” She said with a clenched jaw.

    “It doesn’t matter. It’s either you or him. I don’t have a saying on this.”

    Kara could see she was still keeping her posture protective, her eyes not betraying her body language. She could beg Lena to tell what’s wrong but she wouldn’t tell anything, she was awfully stubborn.

    “Lena, I-I don’t…” She lamely tried to make her understand, she would like to marry her, but not under these circumstances not like this, where Lena would be a hostage in an unhappy marriage.

    “Yes or no, Kara?” She was stiff, unyielding. If she could gain her trust with time, maybe Kara could dissuade her? But now she was expecting an answer.

    “Yes, I will marry you.”

    Chapter Notes

    Alex had ordered enough noodles and sushi to feed an army as she was expecting Kara. Just when she was about to text her to ask where she was, Kara came through the open window, still in her Super suit. She looked depressively pensive, Alex immediately understood something was up.

    “Hey, what happened?”

    She asked, rising to meet with her sister. Kara rubbed the back of her neck and stuttered.

    “I-we,err… I was with Lena, she wanted to know if I still wanted to marry her?”

    Alex’s brows shot up to her hairline.

    “What?”

    Kara started pacing around Alex’s little living room, her cape fluttering behind her.

    “Yeah, exactly! I asked her what changed her mind if everything is OK but she repeatedly asked if I was gonna say yes or no. I mean I knew-I know something’s wrong, she looked distraught and all but she didn’t tell me what is going on! And-and she said if I was to say no to her, she was gonna marry Mon-El anyway so…”

    Kara rambled feverishly and Alex grasped her arms to steady her.

    “Okay first calm down Kara and tell me what happened starting from the beginning.”



    The next night, Lena was about to arrive at her home late, she had tried to spend as little time as possible in the Luthor Corp. She couldn’t stand the victorious face of Lex. He had literally wrapped her around his finger, there was no going back from that. God damn him. She hated him. She hated him so much, under different circumstances she would be happy to put a bullet through his skull.

    She was in the garage of her building, heading towards her private elevator but then she noticed a shadowy figure approaching her. She quickly searched for her stun gun -it was her special design, effective on humans and most of the aliens- and just she was about to face the lurker, the figure spoke up.

    “Miss Luthor?”

    It was a woman’s voice. Lena’s hand was still on her bag, clutching the gun, but she slowly turned to face the woman. If she could reach the elevator, she could call for security-

    “Who are you and what do you want?”

    The woman was an alpha judging by her smell, but it wasn’t an unpleasant smell, it reminded her of the smell of a forest after rain; she had short auburn hair and she was well-built. As she was dressed all black, in combat boots and there was a gun on her belt, Lena betted she was an assassin sent by one of their rivals. Luthors and their enemies. It was not an uncommon thing, she had faced abduction or assassination attempts at least monthly. The woman came to stand a few feet away from her.

    “My name is Alex Danvers, I’m Kara’s sister.”

    Lena was shocked to see the woman, although Kara had mentioned she had a sister she wasn’t expecting to see her on such short notice. Well considering the imminent marriage, it was expected. Still, she did hold onto the gun.

    “What do you want?”

    The woman -Alex- sighed and looked up to the ceiling.

    “It’s okay you can drop your little gun, I’m not here to do anything to you. I wanted to talk with you.”

    Lena’s hand eased a bit but she didn’t fully let it go.

    “Okay, the cameras are offline and no eyes or ears are listening to us, so you can talk to me.”

    She tried to approach Lena, but Lena took a step back. So she’s here for the pity party.

    “I don’t have anything to say to you.”

    Alex stood where she was and sighed exasperatedly.

    “You can talk to me freely Miss Luthor, I know your ‘so called’ engagement to my sister. Also, I know that your brother is planning something and that he’s using you. I’m here not only as Kara’s sister but also as a government agent, if you tell me what’s going on I can help you.”

    Lena snorted and looked at the woman. She looked genuine enough, and probably she had the resources but the risk was too high.

    “Which government agency do you work for Agent Danvers?”

    “That’s a classified knowledge-” She tried to deflect.

    “I’m assuming you are from DEO. Considering your sister is Supergirl, it would make sense.”

    Lena looked at the surprised expression on the woman’s face, she wasn’t expecting Lena to know about DEO.

    “How do you know DEO? Did Kara-”

    Lena cut her off. She was already tired and looking forward to drowning herself with a good scotch.

    “No it wasn’t her. As soon as Kara told me about her civilian identity I did a small background check, you came up as an FBI agent which was a nice cover by the way but I have my resources. Now, Agent Danvers, I would love to stay and chat but there is an expensive scotch waiting for me, so if you’ll excuse me.”

    She tried to turn and step towards the elevator, but Alex grabbed her arm, not roughly just enough to stop Lena.

    “Look -Miss Luthor- I don’t know you; I don’t know if you are a pawn in your brother’s game or-or if you are helping him out; I really don’t care but I care about my sister. She seems to trust you and care for you. One day she’s coming home, tells that she’s going to marry, on top of everything it’s with a Luthor so I think I’m entitled to some answers here.”

    She said angrily. Lena tore her arm from Alex’s grasp and turned to face her to shoot an unimpressed glance.

    “Don’t touch me like that again. I asked your sister if she’s okay to marry me and she said yes. She could have rejected me but she didn’t. So I suggest you talk about this with her. Now, again good night.”

    She started to head to the elevator again, but Alex called out to her again.

    “I know that you don’t want this marriage, I can see it in your eyes. Hell, you guys didn’t even know each other minutes ago. And everyone knows about your brother’s previous arrangement with Daxamites. So I’m betting, your brother is threatening you with something. And you think you can’t fight back, that you don’t have another choice but here I am, telling you; you have a choice. I’m offering you help. I promise I’ll do anything I can to help you, just tell me how I can help you?”

    Lena clenched her fists. She hated being perceived as the damsel in distress. Yes, she was forced but she was not incapable.


    And she didn’t like using Kara this way. She never intended to. She didn’t know her much, but it was clear that she was a good person. Kara had tried to help her every turn these past days, even if she didn’t know Lena. Even if she wasn’t obliged to help her. She didn’t know how broken and alone was Lena. She certainly deserved better. She deserved a better woman than her. Not a broken person like her. She deserved someone who could love her, fully. But here they were. Lena had to force her because she was forced into this. She was bound to her family name, she was a Luthor and she was always gonna be Luthor. Evil and destructive.


    She felt guilty. Especially after she saw Kara’s sister. She felt crushed. Her actions were not only making her life harder, but she was also disrupting other people’s lives. She hated herself because of it. But she couldn’t back off when the life of the most important person for her was in danger.

    She didn’t turn back to face the woman. If she did turn, the woman would see right through her. Alex would see the hesitancy, and she would try to make her talk.

    “There is nothing you can do. Even I can’t do anything Agent Danvers. I-I just have to marry Kara, I must. There is nothing I can do. And for that I’m sorry.”

    Seconds passed, the woman was silent, and Lena was preparing to leave.

    “Do you know Kryptonians marry once in their lifetime? It’s their stupid custom and if you ask me it’s such bullshit, but it is what it is and Kara is bound by it. If you are going to marry her, there is no going back. There cannot be a divorce. You will be using her one shot. You will be married and bound under Kryptonian law. I’m not even mentioning that Kara’s practically royalty. Do you understand? Do you understand the severity of marrying her?” Alex’s voice was much softer but there was a steely gloominess in her tone.

    Lena turned back with what she’d heard. No, she didn’t know about this. She didn’t know once they were married, she would be Kara’s wife, forever. She was using Kara’s only chance of happiness. God, how much do you plan to torment me? she thought.

    “I-I-I didn’t know.”

    She was speechless. She felt her eyes fogging up, her hands shaking. This changed everything and nothing. It was still the same for her but she felt the guilt crushing her lungs.     “No. No, I-I’m sorry to bother you I-”

    Choking back oncoming wave of tears, she started running without turning back. Forgetting her car was waiting for her, she managed to sprint for a couple blocks away.

    She kept running till her lung started to burn and her chest heaved till she reached to a nearby park. Then she slumped down on the grass and found her phone, trying to call Lex with blurry eyes. After a few rings he answered.

    “Lena! How was the reunion?”

    “What have you done you disgusting piece of shit?!” She shouted to phone, a couple of passerby’s heads turned to her way. But Lena kept going on without minding the curious glances. Compared to fury she felt, everything felt inconsequential and faded.

    “If-if you did something to her, to make her forget about me, I swear to god Lex I’ll make you suffer so-”

    Lex sighed through the phone.

    “Calm down Lena, I didn’t do anything. When I first met her, I was shocked too, to see that she didn’t recognize you. She didn’t even recognize dad, so I did a quick search and found out that she can’t remember a part of her life. She’s gone through some therapy, doctors call it dissociative amnesia. I don’t know-”

    Lena hung up on him, with today’s emotional toll she felt absolutely exhausted. It was easy to corroborate Lex’s words and she knew that he wouldn’t make this up, if he did something to her mother he wouldn’t miss the chance of gloating. He was despicable and disgusting but he thrived on boasting what he’d done all the time. She couldn’t even summon the power to cry anymore, she found Kara’s contact in her phone with unseeing eyes and dialed it.

    “Lena, hi! I was just about to-”

    “She didn’t recognize me. She-she can’t recognize me Kara, it’s all g-gone. I-I fucked up, I’m sorry, I’m so sorry-” Hot tears started to stream down again, her head spinning and her vision getting blurry Lena felt her hands going numb.

    “Lena, Lena-where are you? What are you talking about?”

    She couldn’t reply, as her throat constricted, she couldn’t breathe. She panted for seconds, dropped the phone and stood on the grass, on her knees as her vision went black. It was easy to give in to the darkness but still she tried to hold off the exhausting feeling making her head spin.

    She missed the flurry of red and blue catching her just before her head hit the ground.



    To say it was shock was an understatement. Getting a call from a hysterical Lena and frantically searching for her and finally finding her in Ireland. Kara had never been so fast in her life, she had probably broke the sound barrier. For sure, she was gonna get an earful from J’onn and Alex but nothing mattered as she saw Lena like…that.

    She caught Lena just before she fell to ground, leaving a Supergirl-sized crater on the ground she landed. Under curious glances of people, she tried to understand what’s wrong with her as she gently tried to shake her and called her name. Her pulse was getting erratic and she wasn’t responding to so Kara shot up to sky with Lena in her arms, heading straight to DEO.

    With again a record time of travel between continents she was back in National City, DEO as her abrupt landing caused a minor turmoil in DEO, agents stepped aside to open some room. Then Alex and Nia came running towards them.

    “Kara, what the hell is happening?”

    Kara carefully carried Lena to medbay, placed her one of the tables.

    “I don’t know Alex, Lena-she called me and then passed out-I scanned her body everything looks fine but she’s not responding, I tried to-” She said within a breath, her chest constricting with the thoughts of something bad happened to Lena.

    Alex came up next to her and placed a hand on Kara’s arm.

    “Okay-okay, just calm down and I’ll check up on her, okay?”

    Kara retreated just a couple of steps back, to let them do their jobs as some agents came to help Alex. Alex quickly checked her vitals then drew a sample of blood, sending it to lab barking orders.


    Not much time had passed but Kara was pacing furiously, she had an ominious feeling that something bad was gonna happen. Alex had attached an IV to Lena, then she checked the bloodwork results. Kara could see her brows scrunching up and her eyes trailing the results over and over.

    “What Alex? Is she okay?”

    Alex hummed distractedly to dismiss her.

    “Yes, yes she is okay; it’s just malnutrition and some vitamin deficiencies, but…”

    “But what?” Kara tried to peek over her shoulder to results but Alex dismissed her, telling an agent to take another sample of blood.

    “Nothing. I just want to double-check something. She’s okay Kara, calm down.”

    Kara exhaled the breath she was holding. Lena was okay. She was going to be okay. Seeing her unconscious and unresponsive had left Kara worried sick but everything was fine.

    Alex disappeared from the room and Kara went to Lena’s side, she tucked away the loose hairs and caressed her now colored cheeks gently. By the time Alex had come back, she was sitting next to Lena holding her hand, still in her super suit.

    “Kara, we need to talk.”

    She gestured outside, and Kara trailed her.

    “What is it? Is it something bad?”

    Alex puffed out, folding her arms. She looked somber and thoughtful.

    “Kara, were you aware that she is pregnant?”

    Kara stood shocked, her eyes bulging from the eye sockets. How in the name of Rao… She opened her mouth but only a squeak came out.

    “Look don’t be mad, but I need to tell you-show you something.”



    “What the hell she is doing here Alex?”

    Kara said since Lillian Luthor was standing next to them, with a barely disguised anger on her face.

    “I told you we had to keep it from her, why did you call me here Agent Danvers?”

    They were in the conference room, everyone was measuring each other up trying to make sense of the situation. It seemed like Alex was the only one with the full answers and Kara felt her patience running out. J’onn was also there but he stood silent as a ghost, not moving an inch.

    Alex shot an accusing glare to Lillian.

    “You already knew she was pregnant, didn’t you? You didn’t tell me everything and you are still keeping things away from us Lillian. If you want to work together, you have to tell us what’s that you are planning and what’s happened. Spill now.”

    Time stood still for Kara, as Lillian Luthor revealed her son’s sickening plan about using Kara to get Lena pregnant to…He wanted their pups. He wanted to take away their pups from them. He planned everything so that Kara would…

    A half Kryptonian, half human baby. A hybrid. A vessel. That he could shape however he saw fit.

    And Lena. Kara already knew Lex was threatening her with something. She had begged her to tell what it was, that she could help her but Lena was adamant, she didn’t tell anything. So, she did her brother’s bidding to lure Kara into knotting her. To get her pregnant. So that she could give their pup to Lex. Was that the only explanation? It was disgusting and wrong on every level as Kara had exhausted every option in her head but Lena, her Lena wouldn’t plan something like that, would she?

    As Lillian explained more about how Lex was planning to use their pup, how he had been searching for their weaknesses, how he was busy with experimenting on Kryptonians and how he had been working on ‘red sun’ to take make their powers weaken; Kara felt a blind, hot rage casting a veil on her eyes. All of her life, she tried hard to clean up the messes he had caused, never once she had engaged with him as he was cunning enough to not leave his print behind on his disasters. But this- his appalling plan was taking things further, he was challenging her out in the open. It was a direct attack to her, to her family.

    Kara had spent her life trying to control her powers, she was always afraid of hurting others, it was her darkest fear to take a life or to be challenged till she couldn’t see the reason behind being civil. But now she was on the precipice of abandoning that reason. She wanted to hurt him. She wanted to hurt him, bad. For what he had planned, for what he had done. She wanted to kill him.

    “I’m going to kill him.”

    She said as she stood up heading straight towards to door, her hands trembling with rage. Alex blocked her path, holding her arms, J’onn alongside her.

    “Kara, no! You can’t kill people, we don’t kill people; it’s not who we are.”

    Kara held herself back barely not to shove Alex.

    “What do you expect me to do Alex? Didn’t you hear what he had been planning? How long are we supposed to tolerate this? I’m so sick of it, he always gets away with everything. Not this time, this time I’ll stop him. I’ll kill him so everything will be okay. He thinks he can take away my pup, he thinks he can hurt my mate, me…”

    Lillian interjected.

    “As much as I agree with you that we have to kill Lex, Lena is not your concern, she is not your mate. She is a Luthor and my daughter.”

    “She is my wife. She is my wife and she’s carrying my pup.” Kara said with a low growling voice.

    “You haven’t claimed her, have you? And that pup- it’s not a product of a blissful union, it’s the result of some twisted coercion. So it is Lena’s decision to do whatever she wants to do with that parasite. Just because you knotted her, don’t ever think she’s your property-” Lillian said, venom dripping from her voice with an evident disgust on her face.

    “What’s going on here?”

    Lena stood in the door as her eyes had dark circles underneath them, hauling the IV drip with her. She looked between Kara and her mother; as her eyes met with Kara’s she knew she was fucked.

    Oh fuck.

    “What’s going on here?”

But what was her mother doing there, in DEO?

    “Mother, what are you doing here?”

    Her mother diverted her intense glare from Kara to her. She had a dangerous glint in her eyes.

    “It seems I’ve been away for far too long Lena; I’ve heard from Lex that you are a married woman now.”

    Lena scoffed, she felt depleted and battered; she didn’t have the energy to entertain her mother.

    “So he told you that he sold me? Well, I can picture you congratulating his genius. What do you want mother?”

    For a second Lillian’s expressionless face changed into something like…was it, remorse? She looked all steely and neutral then again.

She noticed there were also Alex and J’onn in the room, who were silently judging her with their looks. Alex spoke up, breaking the escalating tension in the room.

    “Look who’s talking about playing god, a Martian and a Kryptonian-”

    “Enough!”

Lena was too exhausted to join into the spat, as she already had a nasty headache and a nausea, but her already erratic pulse sped up after Kara’s outburst. She could guess what was coming by the way Kara looked at her. She refused to cower under alpha’s intense glare.

    “Leave us alone, we have to talk.”

    “Of course not-”

Was- all the things about you getting married to Mon-El…was that a lie? I-I don’t understand. Why did you bother with all that trouble? How could you- I don’t know how did all that happen.” She said tiredly, Lena could see her eyes tearing up as her own eyes welled up too.

And-and then he said, he only wanted me to marry you, that was all, he didn’t want anything more just a simple wedding; I knew it wasn’t all he wanted, I knew he was planning something but still I had to say yes to save my mother-”

    She strained her jaw muscles gritting her teeth to not cry, she had cried enough, and she hated to look miserable in front of Kara, who had always seen the worst state of her already. Feeling weak and useless were certainly fuelling her self-hatred and now she had to explain herself to Kara so that she wouldn’t hate her. The thought of Kara hating her was…unbearable.

But should I do more for you to trust me?” Kara said fervently. She was pacing around and flailing her hands with desperation and anger.

    “Did you-did you help him to send those drones to blow off my powers? So that we’d stuck in that lab and I’d…”

But when it happened I was already…Lex did this on purpose, he knew I’d never let you knot me, at least at this stage of our relationship and he-” She collapsed to a chair, out of instinct her hand drifted to her belly. It was merely a beginning of a life flourishing inside her, but she imagined herself feeling the warmth the of a more-grown belly.

    Kara’s eyes drifted to her belly; her knuckles went white as she fisted her hand.

    “If I didn’t know about this, if-if I wasn’t aware of it now, could – would you terminate without…”

    “I’m sorry Kara, but I wasn’t planning to tell you about it, at least if I couldn’t handle Lex on my own. I don’t think-I’m not ready. I can’t do this.”


    Kara wiped the tears escaping from her eyes, blinked away the onslaught stream threatening to spill. Her heart was too heavy, dragging her down weighing like a ton of brick and she wanted to scream. Why couldn’t she have a bit of normalcy in her life? All of this could’ve been prevented if Lena was honest with her. Did she agree to marry her that forcefully, did she not care about her feeling at all?

    Also, she could see how fragile Lena was, her mother’s life hanging in the balance as she fought so hard over it; she even married a person she didn’t know. She let Kara knot her.

    Kara wanted to shake her from the shoulders, to tell her how bad she broke her heart. But also, the idea of being the reason of the hurt and fear in Lena’s eyes was too much.

    “Is your-did you get your mother back? Is she okay?” She said with a cracking voice.

    Lena’s bit her lips a bit harsh as she tasted blood. Yeah, I got her back, just not like the way I expected.

    “She…she is okay. I’ll make sure she’s okay from now on.”

    Kara nodded her head, her gaze fixed on the ceiling; her lips pressed into a thin line. She wanted to scorch everything with her heat vision, to shot up to sky breaking the sound barrier. She wanted to fly so fast that it’d take her breath away, she wouldn’t be able think.

    “So, what about now? What are you planning to do now?”

    Lena rubbed her temples; her headache was starting to get intense.

    “I’m going to deal with Lex.”

    He was the linchpin. Lex. The source of their problems. Maybe the source of all the problems in the earth. That she could gladly eliminate.

    “No, you are not. I will.” Kara said firmly. She looked at Lena with fiery eyes, her hand hanging in the air indecisively as she reached to her, then she flied off in a flash just before Lena had opened her mouth to protest.
