    Alex folded her arms and squeezed her own biceps. “Then what do you suggest?”

    Lena pursed her lips and looked at Kara.

    “We have to buy some more time. We should gather every Kryptonian out there to an isolated safe space, I can prepare a lab in L-Corp or DEO; it doesn’t matter. We just have to keep away from air till we purify it to a tolerable level.”

    Kara could see the hesitancy in Lena like she wasn’t sure of her own plan which was something she never saw in her.

    “But, how are you planning to purify all air? Wouldn’t it take too long, practically?” She found herself asking. Winn was nodding along with her.

    “Yeah exactly Lena, we - we can’t filter the whole air completely, it’s not efficient and it could take…so much time. We can’t stop the diffusion, it will be all over the US and then the whole world within a day, maybe. And there is the possibility of not getting rid of it completely.”

    “Well I don’t know what more we could do now. We can simultaneously do two things together; a portable chamber construction here which we’ll need some powerful vacuum pumps and creating an isolated space just in case something goes wrong and - and people have to take shelter. But we need more hands either way-”

    Kara was dazed, her thoughts far away as Lena was debating the viability of possibilities, no matter what they do, they were going to be prone to attacks and anything could go wrong. Which would end with them suffocating to death in a Kryptonite-laden atmosphere where they wouldn’t be able to escape. All the options available now didn’t seem enough and viable but…There could be another choice, a cleaner and easier way to evade the incoming mayhem. A choice Kara had alternated long before but Lena wasn’t going to like it. No one was going to like it.

    “The particle’s density is so high so there will be some of them clinging onto everything, but maybe we can use it to our advantage-”

    “I still think we can somehow try to manipulate the timer; we just need to carefully look how to do it without-”

    “What if there is another way? An easier way?” Kara interjected them with a resolute voice. Everyone turned and looked at her.

    “Since nothing will guarantee our safety, and we will be prone to anything coming from Rhea or anyone as we’ll be stuck in some chamber, maybe we can…We will be safe in space. Till an alternative way to handle it could be found. Or any of your solutions wouldn’t work. There are enough ships we all can use. We can go-”

    Alex cut in quickly. “Hell no, Kara. Don’t you remember Nia’s dream? You guys should avoid getting in any spaceships. And where would you go till all of this will be fixed, huh? We don’t know how much time it’ll take and in the meantime, will you drift in space or-or will you go to Argo City? For how long?”

    “I don’t know Alex; it was just a…solution. I don’t know if we will be ever safe, staying here.”

    Lena was looking at with fiery eyes, this was the exact situation she - they wouldn’t want to find themselves in. Kara could see flaring resistance in those eyes but she also could see some kind of comprehension. They had to consider every possibility. She pulled Lena aside to talk privately, she had to assure her that they were on the same page.

    “Lena, look I-”

    “I know what you are going to say Kara. I know, this - this looks impossible to solve right now but…We have to try. We have to try making it right. Earth is our home and - and we cannot give up on it this easily.”

    Kara grasped her hands and looked into her eyes, she tried to channel her emotions through her eyes.

    “Baby, I’m not saying let’s give up on our home, I just…There is a high possibility that nothing would work out. Then we are going to be stuck with Kryptonite-filled air, I can’t stay inside the suit forever and also there are hundreds of Kryptonian refugees. And most importantly we don’t know if it’d hurt our pup-”

    Lena shook her head and furrowed her eyebrows. “I can make something work, I can do it. I just need time but we don’t have enough.” She finished with a defeated voice.

    Kara rubbed her hands. “This will be a precaution, alright? If anything goes wrong and we don’t make it on time, I want us to be in those ships. We don’t have to decide what we are going to do next, we just need to be up there if this thing will go off.”

    Lena looked at her with anger in her eyes which wasn’t directed at Kara.

    “Why would Rhea do this? How can she be so vengeful towards you? I don’t understand.”

    It was something Kara didn’t understand fully herself. The feud between Kryptonians and Daxamites was a thing even before the explosion of their sun, before the explosion of both planets. But with Kryptonians causing irreversible damage to their solar system, they had become the nemesis of Daxamites, of Rhea’s especially. Although they were living on earth together for a while, she was always looking out for any way to harm them. They didn’t have enough power to face Kryptonians face to face, so it made sense that she was orchestrating something catastrophic as this for Kryptonians. Besides their bad blood from years ago, her marriage to Lena had become the final driving force behind Rhea’s revenge plan.

    “I don’t understand too. She was looking for an excuse to attack us till now and once she found one…”

    The traces of guilt in Lena’s face made Kara regret what she had said, she shouldn’t have brought it up. Rhea’s anger towards their marriage was something Lena knew and Kara had noticed that maybe she was blaming herself for Rhea’s irrational hatred. Kara was about to try to console her then Lena looked at the chamber filled with Kryptonite with questioning eyes.

    “How were they able to find this amount of Kryptonite? I know DEO seized most of it…”

    Alex came next to them at that moment, she was looking cautiously at Lena.

    “I can answer to that.”

    Lena sighed exasperatedly. “Not again.”

    Chapter 23

    Chapter Notes

    hello everyone! this is a special chapter bc WE ARE DONE W 100K WORDS! 100K words, can you believe it?!! i cannot tbh

    sooo, emotionally it was hard to write this chap. other than its 100k mark, there are only handful of chapters left till this story is done and through. ugh😣

    anyways, have fun reading and thank you for all the comments and kudos so far! you guys have been great💖 take care!

    Lena was working furiously on the make-shift desk they had given her near the Kryptonite reactor. She had called Jess and tried to tell her she wasn’t going to be in L-Corp for a couple of days, she couldn’t bring herself to say that maybe she wouldn’t be able to go back to her company for a longer period of time which could be months, even years. If she had told her, Jess would want to be there with her but Lena needed her in the company, to oversee the projects and businesses she had left unfinished; Jess was the only one she could trust with her company other than Sam.

    After contacting her lawyers, she had made some sort of an arrangement if things would go sideways and L-Corp would have to have someone taking the helm. The thought of that possibility was spurring her to work harder, faster to fix the approaching disaster but there was a limit to what she could do. Things certainly didn’t seem well, her promise to Kara that she could find a way through this was probably doomed from the start.

    Maybe Kara was right, they being in those spaceships would be better as the construction of Kryptonite-proof doom was already lagging, they were far behind the schedule. Just in case, she had prepared a lab in L-Corp if they were to take shelter in there but as Kara said, it didn’t make any sense. Being stuck in a lab without adequate protection was not one of her brightest ideas.

    Her brain was trying to conjure up anything, anything that didn’t include them being exposed to Kryptonite-filled air and hiding in the space. But really, there was not much of a choice.


    A warm hand on her back caused her to jump slightly and to look back to see who it was. Kara was looking as much haggard as she probably looked herself, the hard lines in her face were more prominent than ever.

    “My love, why don’t I take you home? So you can sleep for a few hours, hmm?” She placed her big hand over Lena’s growing belly and slowly rubbed it in a circular motion. Lena’s back was aching and she’d kill for a couple of hours of sleep, but she couldn’t when there was so much to do.

    She placed her hand on top of Kara’s and leaned her body back to Kara, sighing in relief.

    “I can’t darling, there is so much to do…”

    Kara massaged her shoulders eliciting a strained whimper from Lena, she looked over the schematics and screens scattered around the table.

    “These are not going anywhere Lena and there are dozens of agents working on this. Just, sleep for a couple of hours, I promise I’ll wake you up. Just let me take you home, please?”

    Lena looked around, the place was swarming with agents handling construction and agents still inspecting the reactor. Her eyelids were drooping and maybe a bit of sleep wouldn’t hurt. She looked at the timer, there were approximately 27 hours left. 27 hours till she had to figure this out.

    “Okay but you’ll wake me exactly 2 hours later, okay?” She looked at Kara who solemnly nodded. As Kara opened her arms for her to dive in, Lena didn’t have the power to put up a fight since flying was more time-efficient compared to being stuck in National City traffic. Kara shot up to the sky much slower and carefully than she always did, flew back to the penthouse.

    The moment Kara gently put her down to their bed, Lena hugged Kara’s pillow and smothered her face on it, the exhaustion was lulling her to sleep as she was already halfway asleep. Kara wrapped the duvet around her wife’s body and placed a kiss on her temple, when she attempted to get up Lena drew her back to bed by holding her hand.

    “Mmmn-where are you goin’?”

    Kara brushed her hair off her face and smoothed the creases between her eyebrows with her thumb.

    “I’m going to help them bringing ships and there are many families we have to inform yet, I’m gonna help them to speed up.”

    Lena burrowed herself closer to the pillow.

    “Mmnokay. Don’t forget to wake me up. And be careful.”

    “Okay baby, you sleep tight.” Kara kissed her cheek and went back as she came through the balcony.

    Lena slept but she was restless with the strange dreams she saw, she woke up to her phone ringing. Still groggy from sleeping, she sobered up when she saw the ID of the caller.

    It was bound to be a shit 27 hours.


    Barely half an hour later, she was donning a long coat wrapped around herself as there was a chilly breeze in National City. She shuffled in her place, it was still pitch dark since it was the middle of the night. She sent off a quick text to Kara saying that she was heading over to L-Corp to handle a couple of things so she wouldn’t worry.

    Well, she was lying to Kara, breaking the promise she had set previously but it was something she had to do by herself. Kara wouldn’t let her do it, no one would let her really.

    “I heard that you are in trouble again, Lena.”

    She turned back to greet the looming tall figure.

    “Mother. I didn’t think you would reply back so quickly.”

    Lillian Luthor was wearing head-to-toe black, Lena noted she was lacking the usual sneering and cockiness.

    “I was in town. So, how can I help? I heard something big is going on, so when I saw huge spikes on Kryptonite radiation coming from a place surrounded by DEO agents I put the two and two together. A Kryptonite reactor, huh? I have to admit, it’s clever. And considering the latest skirmish between your wife and Rhea, it’s not hard to guess who’s causing all this trouble. So, I have sent the word out to look for Rhea but I suspect that she is even here on earth…”

    Lena was a bit taken aback by her mother’s willingness to help and how it almost sounded like rambling like she was nervous. Lillian Luthor was a lot of things, but she was never nervous.

    “There is nothing much to do now mother, there are a couple of things we are trying but I doubt those will finish in time.”

    Lillian folded her arms over her chest, her eyes were smoldering.

    “Okay then, what’s the next plan? Did you think of an isolated chamber where you can stay for a while till the child comes out or maybe-”

    “That wouldn’t work mother. Besides, there are hundreds and-and maybe more Kryptonians that need shelter, and labs wouldn’t be sufficient. Kara has been setting up Kryptonian spaceships so we could…be safe in space, away from the earth’s atmosphere.”

    Lena wasn’t sure if she was dreaming but her mother’s face paled considerably and she unfolded her arms.

    “Space? What - where will you go with those spaceships? To Argo City?”

    Lena shook her head and exhaled a shaky sigh. “We don’t know yet but it is a possibility. It seems like the most logical solution right now.”

    Lillian’s mouth opened and closed a few times like she was trying to say something but then she shut her mouth like she gave up. After a disturbing silence, she finally spoke up.

    “You know, if you were to give up on this parasite you wouldn’t need to-”

    “Don’t call my baby a parasite, mother. I’m not going to give up on it, especially not to save my own skin. And even though I wasn’t pregnant I wouldn’t stay here, I would choose to be with my wife.” She replied angrily, the thought of losing her baby to evade the effects of Kryptonite was such a barbaric thing. Lillian’s eyes curiously zeroed on her swollen belly peeking out from the layers she was wearing.

    “Then why did you call me here? To say goodbye? That’s it?”

    There was a bitterness in her tone but it didn’t sound snappish, on the contrary…did she sound sad? If she was indeed sad, Lena couldn’t tell as Lillian had that classical stoic expression on her face.

    Lena steeled herself for what’s coming next, the thing she was going to say was not something easy. It wasn’t easy for her to decide about it too, but now with everything that’d been going on, it was a necessity. She had to.

    “It was Lex who gave Rhea all Kryptonite stock in his hand. Did you know about it mother?”

    Lillian didn’t even blink, she looked completely unfazed. “No, I didn’t know about it but it…It wouldn’t shock me. I knew he had some secret vaults somewhere, keeping his beloved possessions. I was aware that Lex was communicating with someone powerful despite the measures you’ve unsuccessfully taken but-but I didn’t know he was talking with that stupid Daxamite. That…he – they were planning something like this.”

    Lena stabbed her nails to her palm, leaving indents. “I see. Now that there is the possibility of me being away from here for an unknown amount of time, you know I can’t leave Lex in here, unattended, and….it’s the exact opportunity he was waiting for. He would just undo all the things I did and…And I can’t let him do that. I can’t have him chasing me wherever I go, having his breath on me all the time. I-I will have to take care of him, mother, do you understand? For good. Do you understand what I mean?”

    Lillian’s eyes were twitching but her jaw was set tight, she was obviously trying to keep up a neutral face. She stood silent for seconds, the only sounds were rustling coming from nearby trees and honks from cars passing.

    “I…I know this was going to happen. He-he is my son but…he-” Her voice croaked at the end and she averted her gaze. It was the first time Lena was witnessing the hard exterior of Lillian’s crumbling down.

    “Do what you must, Lena.”



    It was still dark outside and the prison looked more desolate than ever, Lena felt the invisible shadows following her through the cold corridors. The guard left her in front of Lex’s cell, he was awake but he looked like he had just woken up; he rubbed his eyes while Lena looked down on him lying on the mattress.

    “Did you decide to torture me in my sleeps too, sister?” He got on his wheelchair with an exaggerated effort and wheeled it close to where Lena was standing. As she stayed silent for seconds just looking at him with her face paler than usual, Lex eventually sighed and offered a small smile.

    “What is it? Couldn’t you sleep? You know, I remember, you used to come to sleep next to me when you were 5 or 6. I despised the thought of a little body clinging to me but I could never turn you down. Well, for a while at least. Do you remember?”

    Lena put her trembling hands to her deep pockets, despite there was premeditation, a preparation she had made; she wasn’t ready to do this. But the bad part was that she was never going to be ready to do this, so the best way was to get it over with. She pulled her smartphone and tapped away a couple of things and put it back, Lex was curiously watching her movements.

    “I know it was you who gave Rhea the Kryptonite. I know that you’ve been talking to her. I told you, I warned you…I never wanted things to come to this point, Lex. Why couldn’t you stay put and…Why did you have to mess it all up, again?”

    Lex clasped his hands over his lap and smiled wickedly like he always did. “Oh, you must have found the surprise. You know, for what’s worth Rhea is not that much of a dumb alien I expected her to be. When I heard her idea, I thought ‘wow, she’s something’. And that Kryptonite you are talking about…Just think of them as your late wedding gift. Oh, and as your baby shower gift, will you?”

    “I’m sorry Lex. I’m sorry, brother.” Lena’s eyes were glistening with unshed tears as she looked down at her brother one last time. Maybe this was the last time she was seeing his eyes shining that bright with all the baleful plans he’d been harboring.

    “What are you talking about? What are you sorry for?” He said with a lopsided smile and at that moment blood started leaking from his nose, slowly at first. He wiped off the blood, smearing it all over his face then he looked back at Lena with a shock. The realization dawning on his face was clear as the day.

    “So you decided to kill me? Is that it?”

    Lena kept looking at him, a few tears falling to her cheek. The blood was now pouring out more and more, even from his eyes, dropping down his t-shirt and staining it unrecoverably scarlet.

    “Are you really going to kill your brother, Lena? Really?”

    His shirt and pants were all stained with blood as it kept flowing, Lena kept her eyes closed as the image in front of her was too much to handle. Lex started vomiting blood and choked on it, the intensity of it had left him breathless.

    After excruciating seconds of hearing Lex wheezing and whimpering, before it all went silent; Lena heard him saying without opening her eyes:

    “Do-don’t t-think it’s over Le-Lena, even if I’m d-dead y-you’ll never b-be safe.”


    Lena stumbled back to the penthouse unsteady on her feet and flopped down on the couch in the living room without taking off her coat and sneakers. The sun was about to go up as there was a beautiful dark blue painting the skies, it looked like Kara’s eyes when she was especially excited.

    She started crying and she didn’t know how much time had passed when Kara found her curled in a ball, lying on the couch.

    “Lena, Lena baby, what’s wrong? What happened, are you okay?”

    Kara tried to untangle her but Lena was keeping her face pressed against her knees, sobbing quietly. She stood silent till her hiccups subsided then she looked at Kara with puffy eyes.

    “He’s gone, Kara. I killed him.”

    Kara was taken aback by the admission, but she was still stroking her back soothingly, trying to learn more about what Lena was talking about.

    “Who’s gone, baby? What did you do?”

    “I killed Lex.”

    Kara stood frozen and looked at Lena to see if she was telling this because of the bad dreams she’d been having or she was saying the truth. Maybe she was seeing one of those dreams again and then in that dream she somehow…

    Lena looked absolutely crushed and battered, she had wrapped her arms around herself and silent tears were streaming down her face. It was not like her previous dreams, she looked…Broken.

    Kara didn’t know what to say, she couldn’t fathom the burden Lena had put herself under by doing such a thing. She could guess her reasonings, Lex had certainly deserved to die a long time ago but as his sister, Lena shouldn’t be the one who did it.

    Kara found herself asking dumbly even if it didn’t really matter anymore.

    “How - when?”

    Lena raised her head and started rambling, tears still leaking from those emerald eyes she adored so much.

    “I couldn’t let-I just couldn’t let him keep hurting us K-Kara, I know I-I should have done it b-before he caused all th-that trouble but he was my brother…”

    Kara exhaled shakily and ran a hand through her hair, they were already having a shitty day dealing with lots of stuff; she couldn’t imagine the emotional pressure Lena was experiencing. She was torn between telling her she did good or telling her she shouldn’t have done it; looking at her small shoulders trembling, her hesitance melted away as she took her sad face between her hands.

    “Hey, Lena, look at me. Look at me, baby. I-I wish, I just wish this hadn’t come to this point and-and you wouldn’t…I understand why you did it, Rao I was on the brink of killing him myself months ago but…I’m sorry that you felt obligated to do it, I’m so sorry you had to go through it alone. I wish you’d tell me how you felt, before-”

    Kara’s phone rang and she had to detach herself from Lena to take the call, it was Alex who’s calling.

    “I guess you know Lex Luthor is dead?” Alex asked with a somber voice, there was a humming coming behind her, she had probably spent the night there, on the complex.

    “Yeah.”

    Alex was silent for seconds then Sam took the phone from Alex and asked: “How is she? Can I come over?”

    Kara sighed and got up from the couch, stepping towards the windows.

    “Not great. I think it’s best we stay at home by ourselves, for a while. We’ll join you guys in the morning but call us if anything changes.”

    She tossed her phone to the table and returned to the couch, opening her arms to hug Lena and to hoist her up to her hips, then strode back to their bedroom and put Lena on the bed again.

    “Kara no, we have to go back-”

    Kara stripped her black DEO-issued clothes and stripped Lena’s clothes too, then she laid down next to Lena on the bed, turning to face her.

    “No Lena, we are going to sleep for a couple of hours then we will go back. Now, come here.” She opened her arms and Lena snuggled herself closer with a sharp inhale, tucking her head beneath Kara’s chin.

    Kara caressed her hair and kissed her forehead as their limbs tangled. She could feel Lena holding herself back not to cry anymore but she shushed her slowly.

    “It’s okay my love, don’t keep it inside, let it all out. I’m here, I’ll always be here. I love you so much.”

    As Lena’s body started shaking again Kara squeezed her between her arms and waited till she cried herself to sleep. She kept patting Lena’s back and never closed her eyes, although she was worried about their future, nothing could be more important than holding her whole world in her arms.


    Everyone was quieter around Lena and she was happy for that, really, she couldn’t bring herself to talk to anyone. But she could feel Nia’s, Sam’s, Alex’s, Winn’s, or everyone’s concerned glares on her from the moment she stepped on the complex, although they were not saying anything, the looks she got was enough for her to submerge herself in the work.

    The doom construction was making progress, albeit slowly, and Kara had informed her that ships were ready to take off and to welcome passengers. Most of the Kryptonian refugees had already started on getting ready, to leave their homes on earth; though everyone was kept hushed at first, someone leaked some details on the oncoming Kryptonite poisoning of the atmosphere which led to a mini uproar not just among Kryptonians, but also among other alien refugees. Although they were stuck in this complex, trying to deal with the Kryptonite reactor situation; they were able to follow the news from the monitors that were set by DEO. The earth was indeed spiraling into chaos.

    Everyone was terrified, there were speculations everywhere saying that it was an orchestrated attack on all aliens on earth, to make them go away. With no one denying these claims, as the clock was ticking, more violence in the body of public protests was spreading out like a wildfire. Lena could see how Kara was beating herself about it, maybe feeling the pressure on herself as she was used to be the champion of both aliens and humans.

    But Lena was determined to make her plan work out, both for herself and for Kara primarily; but also for everyone she cared about. It wouldn’t be just them affected by it, Sam would have to take Ruby with her, Kara was going to be separated from Eliza and Alex, there were also other families that were going to be torn apart and…She just needed to find a way to make it right. For everyone.

    She checked the timer, there were less than 20 hours left. Doom was almost quarterly finished.

    18 hours left. She was nowhere finding something alternative to stop the reaction, Kara was still flying around the globe to warn people and to help the preparation of takeoff. Doom construction was slow, there were multiple labs prepared around the world for anyone choosing to stay behind. Besides the news of riots across the globe, Lex Luthor’s death had brought another wave of questions and mayhem. Lena had started to check the news much less frequently.


    16 hours left. Kara had returned to complex, she was furiously pacing around to make herself useful to anyone. She looked utterly exhausted and despondent. Lena tried to console her, still saying ‘I’m going to fix this’ like a mantra. Sam had also returned from L-Corp, she was busy since the morning to fix her schedule and work stuff; Lena had never seen her this agitated and depressed. Alex had left the complex for a couple of hours, she had promised Sam that she was going to take Ruby from school and bring her to the complex, to her mother’s side. Sam and Alex were going to try to explain the current situation to Ruby but she was most probably already exposed to it by some extent, through social media, it was all over the news. Sam had asked Lena to be there for her when they were to talk with Ruby and she had said yes, but Lena’s heart was heavy with the thought of seeing Ruby unhappy. It was hard already, seeing everyone so glum and hopeless.


    14 hours left. All the Superfriends, even Eliza was in the complex; they were all working side by side. There was a buzzing due to doom construction but other than that, everyone looked gloomy. Thank God, the talk with Ruby went a bit lighthearted than they imagined.

    The first thing she had asked was:

    “Are we going to be alone there mom? In space?”

    Sam patted her hand and shook her head, looking at Lena.

    “No Rubs, your aunt Lena and Kara will also be there with us. And you will get to meet many people at your age too, there will be a lot of people on the ship.”

    “And I’ll be there too Rubs.” Alex had said, shocking everyone.

    “Alex -what?” Sam asked while gaping, Kara was also looking at her incredulously.

    “Alex no, you are not-” She had started but Alex cut in determinedly.

    “You can’t change my mind, Kara. I’ve made my mind since this bullsh…nonsense broke out. I’m coming with you guys and you can’t force me to stay.”

    Kara was still fighting back to convince her. “But Alex-someone has to stay to oversee the rehab process of the atmosphere. Eliza - say something, she can’t just leave everything behind just to be with us-”

    “Well Kara, I was thinking about coming too, but Alex talked me out of it. I think she should go with you and I’ll stay behind, we can work with J’onn to make sure everything will be fixed so that you can return safely. But…of course, I hope it won’t come to that point.”

    “Look, if you are worried about me-”

    “It’s not just you and Lena, Kara. I don’t want to leave Sam and Ruby alone too. They…they are like -my family too and I just found them, I don’t want to-”

    Ruby then ran into Alex to hug her tightly, Lena held herself back barely to not cry because of the touching scene in front of her. Sam had joined Ruby too, and they all hugged tightly till Kara joined into their little circle drawing Lena with her.

    “I guess we are all going into space!”

    Lena had still hopes as she couldn’t help but say: “Not yet, no.”


    12 hours left. Many of Kryptonians that chose to leave the earth with ships started to board on the ships and some of them were spending their last night in their homes on earth. Doom was almost halfway through but Lena and some scientists had raised the question about its stability. Lena was still looking into the reactor, trying to find something overlooked.


    9 hours left. Kara had forcefully dragged her to makeshift bunk DEO placed on the complex, to get her to sleep for at least an hour. Everyone was exhausted but they still kept going on.


    6 hours left. Ships were getting crowded with the onslaught of Kryptonians, some ships had been scheduled to go to Argo City earlier as they were about to complete the boarding process. The team working on the complex was gigantic, Lena was working with dozens of scientists on stopping the reactor. Kara had told her that she had packed a bag for both of them and put it in one of the ships, just in case. Lena had begrudgingly nodded as her hopes started to fade.


    3 hours left. It was clear that doom couldn’t be finished on time. Just a small group of people had chosen to stay in labs scattered around the world; most of the Kryptonians were already on the ships waiting for the lift-off. Kara was anxiously hanging around her till Lena saw a light at the end of the tunnel. A literal light, that could actually work. The moment she told everyone about it, Sam insisted on staying with them till the end. She had to work faster now.


    1 hour left. After having an almost finished plan, they had finally tampered with the reactor without triggering the reaction which was a great success already. Everyone had left all the work they were doing and concentrated on this, it was eerily silent as an operator was trying to take out the motherboard of the reactor carefully. Winn and Lena were in front of their computers in case of an emergency shutdown or anything that could go wrong, Kara and Sam were in Kryptonite-proof suits waiting behind silently.

    Lena felt a drop of sweat trickling down from her forehead as she watched the operator securing the motherboard and shouting an ‘IT’S OUT’ which led everyone in the room to howl with cheering and laughter. Lena wanted to fall back to Kara’s arms and sleep for days but they had to detach the Kryptonite stock from the reactor first. An agent signaled everyone to stay silent as they worked through it for minutes that felt like years.


    20 minutes left was the last time Lena checked the timer and agents signaled they were done with detachment. She got up on shaky legs which were numb with sitting for hours and went to Kara who was looking at her with tears in her eyes. Their bodies clashed and Lena let go of her repressed emotions as she started to cry. People were also crying and hugging each other, patting each other’s back and it was chaotically festive. Lena allowed herself to be hugged by countless others, by a sobbing Nia, a strangely emotional Brainy, an also ugly-crying Winn…She laughed and cried with Sam who was quick to jump onto Alex after that.

    Kara came next to her and wrapped her arms around Lena, whispering to her ear:

    “You did it, my love. You did it. I’m so proud of you.”

    Lena could see Alex was tightly sandwiched between Sam and an enthusiastic Ruby, they looked like a happy little family. She held Kara’s hand over her heart.

    “No, we did it.”

    She was about to turn back and kiss the hell out of her wife, J’onn came next to them almost flying and looking distressed. Lena had never seen him so unsettled before.

    “Kara-Kara, you have to come with me. Now.” He shouted over the commotion, almost dragging Kara with him.

    “What’s going on J’onn? What the hell happened again?” Kara said with a panic in her voice, she was still holding onto Lena who was feeling too weak to experience another bad news.

    “It’s something we have to take care of right now, if we don’t, your mother and all of Argo City could be in real danger…”

    Kara sobered up as soon as she heard about her mother and Argo, she looked back to Lena with tired and fearful eyes. Lena had no choice to send her away.

    “Go. I’ll be fine here. Just go and help them.”

    Kara placed a tender kiss on her temple and shot up to the sky following J’onn, drawing everyone’s attention as Sam, Alex, Nia, Brainy, Winn everyone came up to her.

    “Lena, where did Kara go? What’s happening?”


    Kara could have expected this. She should have thought about it, as Rhea wouldn’t do something without further motivation.

    The ships that took off hours ago, currently on their way to Argo City had noticed there were Daxamite ships following them. A disaster waiting to be unfolded.

    It was great luck that they had noticed it and changed the course back to earth after contacting them. But Kara felt absolutely furious. Rhea wanted them to set off for Argo City, eventually, so that she could send ships to find Argo City and eventually attack their barely surviving planet. It was a great plan, to make them feel trapped on earth with all those Kryptonite and to lure them into going to Argo City so that she could finally find it. The thing she probably didn’t think of was Lena’s brilliance at besting her and saving them from going into space.

    As soon as they had received the transmission from the traveling ships, Kara and J’onn had contacted Kara’s mother, Queen Alura to warn her even though Daxamites didn’t get to learn about the location of Argo City. Alura was seething when she heard what Rhea had done, she even asked for retaliation. Kara was too fed up with everything at that point as she had just assured her mother that she was going to deal with Rhea when the time came. It wasn’t about her being evasive, Rhea was literally nowhere to be found. And they did deserve a little break from all the chaos she had caused. For a while, at least.

    It was a near miss, both on earth and in space. All the ships traveling to Argo were coming back to earth at full speed now that earth was free of danger. Kara didn’t know how much she could handle another attack from Rhea or anyone as she was too battered up, both physically and emotionally. All she wanted was to have Lena in her arms and to sleep. Then to eat lots of potstickers. Dozens, maybe hundreds of them till she couldn’t anymore.

    During the time they were communicating between space and Argo, Kara had informed Lena about what was going on and told her to go back to their home to rest, that she was going to join her later.


    It was a couple of hours later when she was finally returning to the complex, where DEO agents were securing the Kryptonite stock and finishing up dismantling the reactor. Alex was still there but other than her, everyone had left to have a good night’s sleep or a good couple of days’ sleep.

    “Hey Alex, do you need help?”

    Alex looked at her up and down and hugged her tightly, patting her back with a slap.

    “Nope. You look like hell. Just go to your home, to your wife.”

    Kara hugged her back and chuckled tiredly.

    “Wow, thanks for the compliment.”

    Alex shooed her off as she smirked. “Come on, go.”

    “Okay but you too, don’t hand around too much. You need to rest too.”

    With wobbling and hobbling a bit, she finally made it to the penthouse. Immediately, looking for Lena’s and her pup’s heartbeat she trotted around the house but it was ominously quiet.

    She went through rooms and looked everywhere but Lena was not in there. So she hastily took off for L-Corp, maybe Lena was there finishing up something important.

    It didn’t surprise her to find Jess still working but the weird thing was that Lena was not there too. She couldn’t find her heartbeat anywhere after trying to search for it. She called Sam, Alex; anyone she could think of but Lena was nowhere.

    Just she had started to panic and hyperventilate, she got a text from an unknown number.

    I heard you’ve somehow survived my surprises Kara Zor-El, I met with your wife and I can tell she is a special earthling, much better than her brother. And I see that she’s pregnant, you must be thrilled. Let’s see how long it’ll last, shall we?

    Chapter 24

    Chapter Notes
