    “Where the hell is my daughter?”

    Lillian Luthor’s commandeering voice echoed in the DEO’s already crowded control room. J’onn and Alex stepped up and there were several agents pointing their guns at Luthor matriarch.

    “Lillian, how the hell did you get in here? This is a secret government facility-”

    Lillian strode over to them till a couple of agents blocked her path, forcing her to stop. She was shooting daggers with her gaze and she was accompanied by a woman trailing her.

    Kara was exhausted, spent, and fuming for a couple of hours; no matter how hard she had tried to contact Rhea, there was no response from her. They were almost sure that Rhea was on one of the spaceships with Lena but they couldn’t find where they were heading or where they were. So she was on the brink of losing her logical reasonings, it was so tempting to think about annihilating everything that would come in her way. Nothing could stand in the way between her and her family.

    From the second she read that message and searched for Lena’s heartbeat in the entire world, it was like time had stopped for her. It was the second time in her life that she was feeling so helpless, so weak; and the dread of what Rhea could be doing to Lena right now…It was killing her from the inside. It was hurting and it was hurting more than the damned Kryptonite. She was willing to do anything to get Lena back, to have her mate and pup back in her arms; if it meant working with Lillian Luthor and killing an entire army of Daxamites.

    “Alex, J’onn, stop.” Both of them were ready to throw out the tall woman who was looking directly at Kara with an accusing glare and didn’t waver even when Alex stopped just in front of her. Kara felt the overbearing burden of guilt; well, she was right, it was Kara’s fault that Lena was gone.

    “Rhea took her Lillian, we need to-” She tried but Lillian interjected with a vengeful voice, she wasn’t fazed a bit.

    “How the hell did you let Rhea take her? I mean, how could you? Girl of Steel, huh? If you are incapable of protecting your own wife and your child, what use are you to -”

    Alex stepped in with a tense voice. “Hey Lillian, you can’t talk to Kara like this, this was not her fault. We are searching for Lena everywhere, this was no one’s fault.”

    Lillian looked around with a sneer, looking down on everyone till her scorching eyes landed back on Kara. It was the first time that Kara felt Lillian’s anger aimed at her was necessary and rightful, she couldn’t protect Lena. She was so busy with dealing the aftermath of Rhea’s other shitshow, she wasn’t there with Lena when they took her. Her heart was heavy on her chest as she squeezed her hand, the exhaustion compared to that anger she was feeling was nothing.

    “Then who should I blame, Agent Danvers? My dead son? Where were you Supergirl, when Lena was facing her brother alone? Where were you when she even -” Lillian’s voice died down at the end, it was the first time everyone was witnessing the cracks in that evil and icy façade. Only a handful of people knew about the cold, hard fact about Lex’s demise and it was another thing Kara had to live with feeling guilty that she couldn’t there for Lena when she needed her.

    Lillian turned back and pulled the woman trailing her by her arm.

    “Tell them everything.”

    Oh Rao. It was the same woman Kara saw months ago in L-Corp when she got stuck with Lena in that lab that ended up with Lena’s pregnancy. That woman, who lured her into Lena’s lab and closed the door after her. She instinctively took a step towards the woman.

    “I…I know you. I know you from-from-”

    “This is Lex’s little pet, Eve; he was making her do his dirty work for a long time. I found her hiding in one of Lex’s houses.” Lillian said while looking down on the woman with disdain.

    The woman looked around like a frightened animal, she was obviously looking for a way to get out till Lillian shook her from the arms.

    “Don’t even think about it Eve, tell them what you told me or I’ll feed you to those filthy Daxamites.”

    Normally Kara would step in trying to protect anyone who would be in Eve’s position but she was the one who caused all the pain they had to go through and she was Lex’s accomplice, so if she didn’t control herself she would burn the woman with her heat vision-

    Alex touched her arm, causing Kara to recoil a bit. She wasn’t aware that her eyes were glowing.

    “Kara, try to stay calm, okay?”

    She nodded and focused on the woman.

    “I-I was w-with Lex b-before L-Lena came and-” Lillian squeezed her arm causing the woman to squeak.

    “Tell them before that and cut the stuttering.”

    Woman took a deep breath before continuing.

    “Lex w-was talking with Queen Rhea f-for a while after he-he went into prison but-but lately they were talking more through the private channel Rhea g-gave Lex. Lex helped her to get the Kryptonite machine t-to working and-and in exchange for h-his freedom, he told Rhea that Lena was pregnant and-and her child could be an important tool to-to get Kryptonians to do what she wanted. That-that this child could be more p-powerful than Kryptonians, more indestructible than even-even the Supergirl.”

    Her already shaky hands started to tremble and Kara let out a forced exhale, all of the oxygen in her lungs leaving her body. She stood for a second, thinking her Lena alone, scared, drifting in space; while she couldn’t even help her. Lex had done it again, making their lives harder; just as Lena had predicted. But now he was gone, Rhea was nowhere to be found, so Kara couldn’t take out all her rage from anything.

    She punched a nearby table breaking it into million pieces that scattered everywhere with a booming voice. She tried to advance to the woman but J’onn and Sam were next to her in a blink, holding her by the arms from charging at the woman.

    “That-that despicable piece of shit, did he traded her sister for his freedom? Rao, I-I wish he was alive so that I could tear out his spline-”

    Lillian flung Eve to the ground by her arm, a couple of agents rushed to handcuff her and to take her out of their sight. Lillian turned back with a murderous glint in her eyes.

    “You need to give Rhea something she cannot refuse Supergirl if you want to save my daughter.”



    Kara was gearing up, stuffing some artificial sun bombs Lena had specially made for her, she caressed the L-Corp logo with her thumb; her eyes watered thinking about how Lena was doing at that moment. Because of their connection through the mating mark, she could sense some distress and it was making her go crazy, sitting without doing anything. She wanted to see or know if she was okay, they both were okay.

    She had contacted her mother on Argo, she had told about the plan they had made and she warned them -all Kryptonians on Argo to be ready for what’s coming next. It was not one of their best plans, it was a really bad plan but Kara didn’t have time to dwell on more logical ones as the more time passed the more danger Lena was in.

    They had sent out a general transmission to Daxamite ships an hour ago, it was Kara telling Rhea that she was ready to go aboard on her ship to take them to Argo City. She was going to give Rhea her mother and what was left of Krypton, the thing she wanted from the start; something she wouldn’t be able to resist. The first time they had talked about this plan, everyone said it was impossible, that this plan could never work but Kara had hope. She could do it, she barely persuaded her mother to agree on the plan and she had to do it for Lena. And for their pup. Even it meant endangering the existence of Argo City and that of her people. The only good outcome, for now, was that Rhea had responded barely minutes later, telling Kara to come aboard alone. If she were to bring anyone or any weapon on board with her, she was going to put Lena into a Kryptonite-laced room for fun to see how she was going to respond to that.

    Fucking bitch.

    “Kara, you okay?” Sam sidled up to her silently, putting her hand on Kara’s arm. Kara wiped off the tear escaping her eye, she didn’t cry till now and she wasn’t going to start crying now, she needed to be strong.

    “Yeah, I’m ready Sam.” Kara kept stuffing some of the little weapons into her suit knowing that Rhea’s people would probably take them out before she stepped a foot on the ship.

    “You know, it’s okay to let your thoughts and emotions discharge a bit, so that you can-”

    “I don’t have the time for that Sam, I need to get her back.” Kara said with a harsh voice, immediately regretting it. Sam and Alex were there at every step with her, she should’ve been more grateful for their support. Sam looked at her understandingly as Kara's tough face started crumbling.

    “I-I’m sorry Sam, I just need to…I have to…” Tears started to fall as Sam drew her in for a hug, patting her back soothingly.

    “I’m concerned as you are Kara, you know that she’s a like a sister to me, like you and Alex.” Sam softly said while Kara kept crying on her shoulder, hanging on to her like a koala.

    “I-I know Sam, I’m sorry- you have been nothing but helpful since the start and I-I never properly thanked you for anything, I’m sorry…”

    Sam put a distance by holding her arms, she looked directly to her eyes; Kara could see nothing but compassion and sincerity in those friendly hazel eyes.

    “There is no need to thank me, Kara, I would do anything for Lena and now, for you. We are a family and it’s all going to be okay, trust me. We’ll get Lena back safe and sound.”

    Kara nodded wiping the tears in her eyes, she and Lena were so lucky to have such good friends in their lives. Sam was right, everyone in the room working hard to save Lena was family; Nia, Winn, Brainy, J’onn…Most of the time, the blood didn’t matter at all to feel at ease with people, it was the chosen family that made everything better.

    “Alex had taught me something, correct me if I say it wrong; El Mayarah?”

    Kara bit her lips to not cry more, she had already unleashed a substantial dose of pent-up emotions; hearing her family motto from Sam was tipping her back to cry some more. They haven’t noticed Alex had come near to them:

    “I think I’ve heard my name. Kara, I hope you are behaving near my fiancée.”

    Kara dumbly nodded till she noticed what Alex had called Sam…did she say, fiancée?

    “What? What did you just say to Sam?”

    Alex trotted up, came next to them, wrapped her arms around Sam’s back, and smiled at her.

    “Well, I might have asked Sam to marry me and she might have said yes…”

    Kara gasped loudly and brought her hands to her mouth in shock, it was a shock. But a very pleasant one.

    “WHAT?! Oh my Rao, I can’t - Wow- I’m so so happy for you guys! It’s such wonderful news, Lena’s going to be thrilled when she hears about it…”

    Alex and Sam both nodded their heads looking a bit downcast upon hearing Lena’s name but Alex hugged Kara tightly till Kara wondered if Alex had gained some powers.

    “Mom’s been wanting to talk to you, maybe before we lift off you should go and…” Alex said unsurely while withdrawing herself back.

    “Okay, I’ll find her.”


    It was a mess, inside the DEO. Agents were running around trying to prep the hangar, Winn was trying to predict the route of Rhea’s ship, and Nia, Brainy were trying to help him; J’onn was trying to make something of an attack plan for later if they would be able to make contact. But it was a long shot, no matter how much they prepared themselves, Rhea’s ship would have the upper hand, also there was the possibility of other ships in her fleet safeguarding the main ship. So, everything seemed a bit hopeless at this point to everyone but still, they kept working.

    Kara saw Eliza talking to some agents and rushed to her side, Eliza detected her immediately. Eliza had come after the incident with the Kryptonite reactor to help them, her presence was an immense support for both Alex and Kara.

    “Kara! Are you ready for the lift-off?” She drew Kara in for a hug which Kara gladly obliged, Eliza’s hug was one of the best.

    “Yeah, I’m ready Eliza.”

    Eliza looked with a loving and concerned expression till Kara sighed and relented. Sometimes she was feeling like Eliza could see right through her lies, just like a mother would do.

    “Okay, I’m so scared and-and nervous. I just don’t want anything happening to them.”

    Eliza stroked her arms. “It’s alright to be scared Kara, but don’t let your fear lead you to do something you’ll later regret, okay? Just go in there and bring Lena back safe.”

    Kara nodded mutely, she knew why Eliza was worried about her. Whenever she had these dark thoughts screaming for mayhem Eliza would notice it by the look of her face, she would try to steer Kara out of trouble. She was usually successful at it, Kara had always tried to suppress her aggressive side, stuffing all the violent thoughts that were threatening to burst in her head.

    But not this time. Because this time, Kara’s mind was set as she promised to herself and Rao that there was no way Rhea and her army were coming alive out of that ship.



    Lena opened her eyes to unfamiliar surroundings, she was lying on the bed in a room she had never seen before. The last thing she remembered was that she had just entered the penthouse, she was about to head shower before falling off to bed for a good sleep, then - then there was a very bright light coming from-

    With a whirring sound, the room door opened and revealed a tall, beautiful-looking brunette striding up to the bed, next to her. The woman was wearing a crown and there was a huge flashy necklace on her neck; it was no one other than Queen Rhea of Daxam.

    Although Lena had only seen her pictures before, it wasn’t hard to guess who she was. Also, her being an alpha was convenient with the way she was looking at Lena like she was a piece of meat; Lena stopped herself from shivering upon smelling Rhea’s aggressive pheromones. If she wasn’t mated, she’d certainly choke on that musky alpha stench.

    They were looking for her everywhere just until yesterday, she was the damn source of their troubles for these past days, even past weeks; now she was standing just a foot away from where Lena was lying on the bed. What the hell.

    “Rhea, where are we? What-what am I doing here? What do you want from me?” She instinctively balled herself into the corner of the bed away from her, holding her pronounced belly. It was hard to look for something that could be used as a weapon as she was still a bit disoriented and with her hands’ movement, Rhea’s eyes drifted her belly.

    “It’s finally nice meeting you Lena Luthor, I wish it was under different circumstances. I’ve heard so much about you and I have to say, you are quite…surprising. I expected a harlot of an omega but…Well, let’s say you seem more resourceful than I imagined. I would have wanted you to be my son’s bride but that damned Kryptonian had to mess that up too-”

    Lena felt dread drilling a hole in her stomach, there were three things she had noticed while Rhea was babbling some stupid monologue; 1. she was on a spaceship, 2. from what little she could see from the door there were guards in her door and 3. she didn’t know where Kara as if she was safe.

    “Where is Kara? Did-did you do something to her?”

    Rhea sat on the bed elegantly folding her long royal red dress, she offered a small smile.

    “Not yet, Kara Zor-El is on her way here, she’ll be joining us shortly I presume. You have been sleeping for a while.”

    Lena tried to look strong without showing her fear to Rhea who looked reserved now but from what people had told about her, she was brutal enough to play on people’s insecurities. She couldn’t show her how frightened and vulnerable she felt as she was left at Rhea’s mercy, only if she could tour around to find something she could use-

    “What are you going to do to us? What do you want Rhea?”

    Rhea kept smiling without saying anything, she fixed her glare on Lena’s belly.

    “You must be thrilled for this pup Lena, I remember how happy I was when I learned that my husband was pregnant with Mon-El.”

    Lena kept her hands on her belly, her body balled up in a corner avoiding contact. She didn’t say anything and Rhea watched her like a hawk for a couple of seconds before standing up.

    “Rest now Lena, there’ll be a long journey ahead of us.”

    Lena opened her mouth to ask what she was talking about but Rhea turned back and went away as she came. As soon as the door closed after her Lena sprung up to her feet, her head spinning a bit with the sudden movement. It had been some time since she had eaten something, just then she saw a plate left on the desk by the door. There were some fresh fruits and crisp-like-looking dry biscuits, it looked okay but Lena couldn’t know if Rhea had injected something into them so she chose to try her chances when her hunger was too unbearable, later.

    She looked around the room, there were a couple of small windows offering a view of the breathtaking space view. It looked absolutely dark, enigmatic yet frightening. Since she was a little girl, she wanted to go into space but she couldn’t have imagined this would be her first time in space. They had many projects in L-Corp developed for space, they were sending out regular cargo to some nearby planets like Mars and Moon, Lena had every chance to accompany them to one of those missions but because of her desk duties, she never thought about it. Now, this, as a prisoner on a Daxamite ship was her first time in space and she didn’t even know if they were still close to the earth.

    Going around the room, she tried to find something, anything that she could use to deliver a message back to earth or to wield as a weapon but apart from the bizarreness of the technology of the ship, they were careful enough not to leave anything useful behind; her options were just some weird looking clothes and a plate of snacks. It was a spacious room with a big bed in the middle, there was a minimal bathroom attached to it and that was it. There was nothing else in that goddamned room.

    Why had Rhea said Kara would be joining them soon? How - why was Kara coming onto the ship? Was she okay? What was going on, where they were headed to? Lena had dozens of questions swirling in her head, she was feeling exhausted despite waking up barely minutes ago but she didn’t want to sleep again, she couldn’t know if they would transport her to somewhere else or if they’d do something to her, to her baby…

    She leaned her head back on the headboard, struggling to keep her eyes open. She resisted but soon the sleep took over as her head fell on the bed again.


    When she opened her eyes the second time still in that fucking room, it was because there was a commotion outside; there were some yelling and booming sounds shaking the ship. Lena straightened herself on the bed and went for a window to look outside, they were moving fast from a point-like looking thing…was it a ship? Did someone get on board? Was it Kara?

    She paced around the room, tried to remove the panel next to the door but it was an arduous task as it was wielded safely to the steely structure that was the wall of the room. She tried to listen to voices and banged on the door trying to summon someone - anyone that would hear her voice but there was no response back. Hours later as Lena was mentally calculating how much time she’d spent in between there was a movement outside her door, a set of footsteps then the whirring sound of the door’s opening.

    Lena stepped back and then Rhea strode in angrily, two guards following her. She stopped a few meters away from Lena, her face was red and she looked beyond irritated, Lena steeled herself for what could come next.

    “I need you to come with me, your wife needs some convincing and I can’t kill her, not yet.”

    Kara. So it was Kara who boarded to ship.

    She needed to see her as soon as possible, to make sure Rhea didn’t do anything to her and to show that she was okay.

    Lena nodded hastily as she followed Rhea through the maze of corridors, with guards hot on her trails. If she weren’t hurrying to get to her wife, she would try to memorize every turn, every room she saw on her way but the only thought in her head was to see Kara.

    On a lower level compared to where they were keeping her, they entered a big room where there was a transparent cage in the middle. Kara was hunched over the ground, holding her ribs; Lena rushed to her side without minding guards’ protests. She heard Rhea mumbling ‘leave her’ as she got on her knees to look better at Kara, who was unresponsive to their entrance since she was coughing up blood to the ground.

    “Kara? Kara, darling are you okay?”

    Kara’s head snapped at her so quickly which made her whimper slightly but she crawled up to where Lena was kneeling on the floor. Lena felt her heartbreaking seeing Kara like that, her beautiful face bruised but her eyes glimmering with excitement the moment she saw her.

    “L-Lena? Are you okay? Did they do something to you? To our baby?”

    Lena’s emotions were overflowing upon seeing that tenderness, anxiety in those clouded blue eyes, she lifted her hand to lean it on the glass but Kara interjected hurriedly.

    “No! No Lena, don’t touch, there’s Kryptonite covering on the glass-”

    She had never contacted Kryptonite before, especially after learning that she was pregnant so they didn’t know if it was harmful to the baby, they were not so keen about finding what it could do.

    Lena turned back to look at Rhea who was watching their interaction with a bored face, if she had a lead somewhere she wouldn’t hesitate to beat Rhea with it.

    “Let her out of there.” She hissed.

    Rhea looked unimpressed with her warning tone. “She tried to cause problems even though I had told her to come in peace, for your sake. But considering all Kryptonians are thick-headed insects, it was expected from her to try something like this.”

    “Bring the absorber.” She said to some guards who brought a big device that looked like a neural helmet with tangled wires and a protective visor on the front. It looked awfully similar to what Lex was working on for a while, an entrapment device he could use to blow out Supergirl’s or Superman’s heat vision so that they could solar flare, on a miniature scale. Maybe Lex had given it to her or maybe she had stolen it from his secret vaults. Not that it mattered now, with Lex already dead.

    “I can let her out if she exhausts her powers to this absorber. Otherwise, she is going to stay in this cage, locked, till the end of our journey. She refused to do it but maybe you can convince her.” Rhea said with an amused voice. Kara growled at her, she was still doubled over on the ground but Lena could see the murderous sparkle in her eyes; if it weren’t for the Kryptonite cage she would have incinerated Rhea and the ship by now.

    She was obviously uncomfortable, her breath was coming out as puffs with being in close proximity to Kryptonite. They didn’t have much choice - them being apart on the ship they knew nothing about, their destination also vague, they needed to stick together although Kara wouldn’t have her powers and Lena would have no access to tools she could utilize.

    “Can you leave us alone?” Lena said with a strained voice, they were walking on a thin thread with Rhea who showed almost a good hospitality towards her which was surprising as it was. But she could turn into a blood-driven maniac any second with a tiny gesture or word, and she had the upper advantage.

    Rhea pursed her lips but nodded curtly, she left the room, and also most of the guards trailed her leaving the room but two of them waiting by the door, far from the cage.

    Lena turned to Kara, who had quitted the brave front she was showing in front of Daxamites, now wheezing with Kryptonite contaminated air. Lena could see the green veins scattered across her face.

    “Kara, you are going to tell me what the hell’s going on but first, you need to get out of this cage. It’s-it’s poisoning you slowly, I can see it.”

    Kara coughed up, her shoulders slouching. “Baby, they took all the sun bombs and I don’t have anything with me, I-I don’t know how I can protect you once I give them Argo City’s location and they-they must be far behind us…”

    Lena recoiled upon hearing Argo City, so this was all it was about. Rhea wanting to get to Kryptonians, trying to find all of them so she could get rid of them all at one go. But how - why Kara was giving off such a well-kept secret-

    “You cannot tell them about Argo City Kara, Rhea would-”

    “It’s all been decided, Lena. In exchange for your safety I-I’m going to tell them about Argo’s location. We made a deal with Rhea, I offered it at first.”

    Lena looked at her incredulously, how could she do such a thing? How did even her mother-Queen Alura allow this to happen? Didn’t Kara know she was risking the lives of her people?

    Lena had so many questions but though it seemed like a bad idea, Kara losing her powers seemed like a better option than having her suffocated with Kryptonite. Kara seemed to murmur something to herself, she was counting something with her fingers while nodding her head.

    “Kara, you are going to-”

    “Okay baby, tell them I’m going to wear that thing so I’ll solar flare. I’ll do it.”

    Lena looked at her quizzically, nothing she was saying made sense. Obviously there were some other things Kara was keeping to herself as her eyes scanned the roof of the cage and the walls like she was suspecting that someone was listening to them.

    Rhea came back to the room, looking smug to see a Kryptonian admitting defeat. “Are you going to do it then, Supergirl?”

    Kara stood up in the cage on shaky legs wobbling, she raised her chin defiantly. “I will. But to make sure nothing happens to me before we arrive at Argo, I need some reassurances. So I’m not going to tell you the exact location of it, I’ll give the coordinates of a close star instead, we’ll go from there.”

    Rhea’s face fell and her face muscles began to twitch. “We haven’t talked about it before, I won’t accept it.”

    Kara shot back without missing a beat. “You also didn’t tell me that you were planning to torture me with Kryptonite but here we are.”

    “You are missing the point Supergirl, I have your wife and your pup on at my mercy, you have to do everything as I say-”

    “You can try to kill them- kill me, but once you do, you won’t know where Argo is and you will drift in space. You can’t even go back to earth or to anywhere, there will be ships looking for me- for us. You will be hunted, your son will be hunted, your species will be hunted. And once I solar flare, I can’t do anything without my powers, isn’t that enough insurance for you?”

    Rhea snorted and frowned at her. She seemed like pondering over for few moments.

    “If you are stalling me, I will kill your wife in front of you with your pup then-”

    “I’m not stalling you. Once we go there, you will be able to see Argo, you will see.”

    Rhea smiled ominously. “Bring the helmet.”


    Lena was in Kara’s arms, back in the same damned room; they were both crying and hugging each other.

    “I thought-I thought she did something to you :i zhao, I-I thought-”

    Lena dried her eyes and forced Kara’s face back to look at her. She looked so devastated with the prospect of them being hurt, Lena had to assure countless times that she was okay that they didn’t touch her.

    “I’m alright my love, we are alright…”

    Kara put her hand above her protruding belly and sniffed between her tears. When she looked back at Lena, there was pure love in her eyes.

    “You know, both of you are my whole world, right? You are my everything.”

    Lena combed her free hair off of her face, she looked around and lowered her voice to minimum.

    “Kara, we have to talk about Argo, are you really going to take them-”

    Kara held her hands and squeezed them. “It’s okay Lena, my mother’s already expecting us, and Alex, Sam, J’onn-everyone is following us with Kryptonian ships. We just have to get through these couple of days and-and-”

    “Then what?”

    “I’ll make everything alright, I promise.”

    Chapter 25

    Chapter Notes

    hello everyone, hope everything's ok with you!

    we have 2 more to go and then...🥺🥺

    this chapter especially has a special place in my heart, it was hard to write; heavy on emotion, action-filled...i hope you will like it.

    thank you so much for your support, to everyone reading this fic and leaving comments, kudos💛 have a nice sunday!

    It was surreal. And excruciating.

    Spending days with Kara, on a Daxamite ship, drifting in the space. It was the last thing Lena thought she could experience out of all the bizarre scenarios in her head.

    Kara looked surprisingly calm with everything, maybe too calm to be considered as normal, Lena was getting worried about her with every passing second.

    Rhea was making her presence known every day by stopping by to insult Kara and her family, she was always trying to rail Kara up but it was fruitless at every turn, Kara was unresponsive to her attacks. Even it was Lena who was occasionally fuming with anger, lashing out to Rhea’s face to make her shut up, Kara was coaxing her to cool off every time. She was bottling up her anger inside and it was unnerving, Lena was afraid that the imminent outpouring of Kara’s emotions would be…catastrophic.

    Also, although she had told Lena about their escape plan and getting through the whole thing with Daxamites safe and sound, Kara was obviously keeping some things to herself as Lena could see her mind tuning out occasionally. Their plan was half-baked, prone to fail with the slightest change. But Kara looked steely resolute, she assured Lena many times that it could work, that they could survive it together. Looking at those blue orbs she loved maybe more than herself, Lena had no choice to believe in her. Kara’s mere presence meant everything to Lena, no matter how deep shit they were in.


    It was hard at first, having Kara with her certainly decreased the level of anxiety she was feeling but now Lena was fearing for both of them, about how to get out and what to do next since they were heading towards the Argo City at full speed.

    Seeing Argo City, and the possibility of meeting Kara’s real mother -Queen Alura- was a frightening thought for Lena as she mostly stopped herself thinking about it, they needed to get off the damn ship first. But she was incredibly nervous because they were never introduced properly and Lena was almost sure from what Kara had told about her, it was highly likely that she wouldn’t get along with her mother-in-law. Which wouldn’t be a surprise considering how Lillian and her biological treated her.

    Some things like how they were going to get rid of Rhea before they’d set foot on Argo or how Kryptonians were allowing such intervention to happen were still unclear to Lena, she wanted to demand some answers from Kara but talking about their plans out in the open was not logical, Rhea could be spying on them, although the first thing Kara had done was to search the room they were staying for anything out of the ordinary.

    Kara was always the first one to try out the food Rhea was sending to test it to tell if it was poisoned, then she was allowing Lena to have it. The food was nothing special, it was enough to keep them nourished. Considering the circumstances, Rhea was incredibly hospitable towards them, till the point of suspicion; but maybe she was grooming them, till the time she was going to kill them, probably in front of an audience. It was a harrowing thought, and Lena wanted to believe -she believed in Kara that they were going to be okay but with the clock ticking and them getting closer to Argo, she was running all the possible outcomes in her head but not heavily leaning on the statics, for the sake of her sanity.


    After getting used to the routine they had created, it was much easier; the days that seemed infinitely long had turned into long cuddles in the bed talking to each other with whispers and making plans about the future, it was their perfect escape from the storm that was brewing in the horizon, a perfect distraction.

    Kara had told about her plans on moving into a bigger house in the suburbs when they got back to earth, a suburban house with white fences, mailbox, and all that classical jazz came with the American dream, for after their pup was born. Lena had just silently listened with her eyes watery first, but then she had told Kara about the house she was dreaming of since she was a little girl, how they could make a home for themselves. The topic had even drifted to Lena’s mother whom Lena left back in Ireland, with a secret army of people she had arranged to protect her mother from afar. When she recalled the events that brought them together and separated them, maybe indefinitely, Lena couldn’t help but broke down in Kara’s comforting arms.

    “I would want her to remember me and - and I would want her to be in our life. I just…I want that with my whole heart, you know? I would want her to know her grandchild, but I know it’s just a…pipe dream. She’s better off with her memories gone, much safer. And much happier.” She had said after crying her eyes out as Kara stroked her back soothingly for hours till she was asleep in her arms.


    Kara was barely sleeping as she was alert all the time, a little sound was enough to irk her. Behind all the bravado and the aloofness, Lena could see the tenseness in her figure, she’d told Lena that it was the first time she was traveling in space after all those years she spent stuck in a dark place where time was frozen, the Phantom Zone as they called it.

    It was the first time she was talking about her time in Phantom Zone and back in Krypton before it exploded. Lena did know about the horrors she went through but hearing them from Kara was different, it was unfiltered and helping her to shed new light on Kara, to get to know her more. And also seeing her usually cheerful, protective and strong wife full of cracks, was tearing her heart and making her want to comfort her.

    “I…I have never told this to anyone, not even Alex but…when I heard my mother was alive, on Argo even after the explosion and that my father was dead…I felt relieved. I wasn’t -I didn’t feel good about it. It was my father who experimented on Rao, our sun, and our god, the things he did led us here because-because he was greedy and prone to be influenced by others and I always blamed him for everything. I know, I shouldn’t have but still…And it was him who saved the rest of my people, after all. But I spent 24 years of my-my life there, in the Phantom Zone, I wasn’t awake during the whole time but when I remember how trapped I felt in the pod, how alone and scared I was…I blame my father for that too. And maybe my mother too. That’s why I-I felt like…I was given a second chance in life when my pod landed on earth when Danvers took me in. I have my best memories on earth, with people I call my family and-and more importantly, I got to meet you. I found you.”


    They both were broken and looking for a way to heal. It had started long before they had found each other, for both of them; for Lena, it was since the day she was brought to Luthor mansion and for Kara, it was since the day she had to leave her planet. With everything building up along the way, they were exhausted. They were heavy with insurmountable burdens. Talking about their struggles, their dysfunctional families, and bad thoughts; it was a big step. A good step on the way to be better, together. Not to cure each other but to cure themselves in the company of each other. It was the thing making them connected, beyond the mating mark they were sharing, beyond anything physical.


    It was such a funny thing, they never had the chance to talk that much, back in the earth as they were both battling with different problems in their lives; now they were practically trapped together, they had all the time to talk things through and to spend time without doing nothing.

    Everything was frightening yet, Lena felt the glimmer of hope warming her heart up with the prospect of things would be okay, that they were going to be okay. For that, they needed their plan to work and then they could find their way back to earth.

    And for that, they needed to leave the goddamn room too.

    The only time they were leaving the room was when Rhea was summoning Lena to her stupid, little throne room to make some talk about the projects she was overseeing in the L-Corp. At first, they thought it was a little provocation, which Rhea was testing boundaries but it proved to be wrong, seemingly she was really interested in the work Lena was doing on earth, how she was building tomorrow’s technology.

    She was a businesswoman, just like Lena, and with their chats, Lena could see that Rhea was passionate about making a home for her people, devising plans to make her people evolve and her planet thrive. The problem was that she was planning to build her dream home on top of what Kryptonians had built already, she was seriously considering mass extinction for a fresh start even though it meant killing countless innocents and annihilating a culture.

    Rhea’s fascination with Lena was something deeply troubling Kara as she was always insisting on accompanying her wife, even when Rhea was making her wait at the door like a guard dog, she was making use of that time trying to memorize all the possible exits of the ship unbeknownst to Rhea. It was the only time Kara was feeling her tough exterior crumbling slowly since she was detesting the idea of Lena being alone with Rhea, she wanted to burst through the doors and punch the daylights off the Daxamite Queen.


    On the approximately fifth day they were on the Daxamite ship, Kara was lying on the bed with Lena’s face leaned on her heart; her eyes were fixed on the ceiling, she was trying to estimate how much time had left and how should she try to tackle the guards without Rhea being-

    Lena stretched out and pulled Kara by the chin to make her look.

    “Why don’t you sleep a bit, hmmm? You look really exhausted darling.”

    Kara squeezed her hand and kissed it. “I can’t today, baby. Today is the day.”

    Lena straightened herself on the bed, looking towards the door. “Is it? This soon?”

    Kara followed suit as she leaned back with a sigh. “Yeah, we must be close to Argo. Maybe a day away.”

    Lena picked on her nails and thought about all the things Kara had said, how Alex and Sam was going to come to their rescue, and then there would be Queen Alura joining with Kryptonians later - but first Kara needed to get her powers back, the whole plan was dangerous and half-planned and maybe Kara couldn’t get her powers back before Rhea knew something was up-

    “Hey, baby? Look at me.”

    Her heart was beating faster and with the unknowns swirling around them, Lena felt the reverberations of small quakes signaling a bigger disaster for them, there were things that could spiral out of the control so easily. The whole thing was based on many assumptions and although Lena loved to come up with assumptions that facilitated her work in the lab, in reality, assumptions were scary. Assumptions meant almost certain failure. And now their lives depended on it.

    Kara leveled her gaze and caressed her cheeks, lowering her voice.

    “There is nothing to be afraid of, okay? It’s gonna be alright and when the time comes, I want you to listen to me, to do exactly as I say; can you promise me to do that baby?”

    Lena searched her face for hesitation and fear but she found none, Kara looked absolutely determined as if she wasn’t going to start a literal war barely hours later.

    “What about your powers Kara? What if you can’t get them back in time, what then? You told me you needed them, especially when Alex’s-”

    Kara interjected with a convincing voice. “It’s okay Lena, we can still do it. They took the sun bombs I brought with me, and I have an idea where they are keeping them. When we will be returning from Rhea’s to here, I’m going to knock out some guards and we will get to the sun bombs but we have to be quick. There will be one shot at this and we have to make sure you are beyond her reach, okay? Beyond their reach, your safety is our first priority. Always think about yourself first, no matter what happens my love. Promise me that you are going to put your safety above anything else, please?”

    It was suspicious the way Kara was trying to coax her to stay safe in a scenario in which a lot of things could go wrong. Lena knew Kara would be relentless if she were to probe further, to learn the things in Kara’s mind. So she just nodded and relented. “Okay, I promise.”

    Kara drew her in a tight hug and they lied on the bed, limbs entangled till the time came for them to go to Rhea’s side, with the usual two guards that were accompanying them. They were armed and possibly carrying Kryptonite, but both of them were a bit reckless as Kara had observed them, there could a be window of opportunity for her to strike.


    When she was waiting by the door for Lena, she traced the path to the small space she’d briefly seen other day, where they were keeping her stashed sun-bombs, hidden in the glass case she had brought with her when she boarded to the ship. Daxamites went through the case but they couldn’t find the sun bombs, there were only the glasses Lena had made for her and it looked totally harmless. At least to them, since they didn’t know Lena had made some upgrades on her old glasses. So, if she could get that case, where they left it at the open, lying there…

    The door opened and Lena came back, with additional two guards following her.

    Shit.

    Kara could take out two guards which was also a maybe - but four guards, heavily armed, tracking her every movement…It was going to be hard. Very hard.

    Lena’s face looked paler than usual, her eyes were unfocused as she sidled up to Kara.

    “K-Kara, t-there is something wrong-”

    Kara clasped their hands together and started walking, she was internally debating whether they should wait for DEO’s ship to approach first, it could take a long time as their original plan was for her to create a distraction, then Alex and Sam coming bustling down to get them out-

    “Kara.”

    Kara’s attention shifted to Lena’s face, her voice was strained as she was puffing out a forced breath with her free hand on her belly. Just then, Kara noticed the way green veins slowly spreading out in her face, very slowly. Her heart stopped. What the-

    “Lena, Lena what’s wrong? What happened? Did-”

    Lena could walk and talk but she had started to sweat and her face was contorted in pain.

    “R-Rhea, she injected me something and s-said she’s going to give the antidote once we reach Argo now that we are getting close. She s-said it wouldn’t kill me and-and our pup by then…”

    Her steps faltered as she looked at Lena, her hands felt heavy, her brain was foggy; Kara looked back to where they came, she considered sprinting back and stabbing that monster of a woman right in the eye but she couldn’t make it without her powers.

    The plan was to take Rhea to Argo’s orbit where they could distinguish the planet from afar but before Rhea had any chance to attack or try to land on Argo, they were going to get rid of them, completely. They just needed to survive till the checkpoint but now that she had injected something into Lena, Rhea was taking no risk. Or she thought she wasn’t.

    Despite the crippling anger she was experiencing, Kara had no choice but to go with the plan, Rhea was bound to lash out once she saw a flock of Kryptonian ships coming onto them.

    She needed to get her glasses to get her powers back, immediately; no matter how impossible it looked. Once DEO’s ship was near, she could send Lena with Alex and Sam back to Argo so they could try to find something that could fix Lena-

    Or she could take the antidote by beating the shit out of Rhea, which would be a near-impossible beat, considering she had to go through all the Daxamites on the ship before she tried to do anything to Rhea.

    But first, she needed that glass case and Lena off the ship.

    She leaned in and lowered her voice. “It’s okay my love, nothing’s gonna happen to you, I won’t let anything bad happen to you, I promise. Just - just when I squeeze your hand, let go and run towards that corridor, alright? Don’t look back, I’ll be behind you.”

    Lena’s eyes scanned ahead till she saw the cross-section of two corridors, where they were supposed to ditch the guards to go to the small room. She nodded and they kept moving with guards, two of them were in the front and two at the back; Kara counted down to herself, she needed to be quick, much quicker than in her training with Alex back at DEO if she wanted to survive. Be quick and merciless she chanted.

    When she squeezed Lena’s hand and immediately let it go, she reached for the closest guard’s gun, jumping back at him by holding his neck and twisting it till it cracked so that his body could shield her. Before two guards at the back found the time to draw their guns and point at her, she fired the gun many times, a couple of shots piercing through the armor they were wearing as they thudded to the ground. The remaining guard fired his gun but his shots hit the guard Kara was holding, then she shot him and sprinted after Lena towards the corridor.

    Everything felt like in slow-mo but in reality, they were moving fast as normal people could do.

    Sirens started to blare as shouts and footsteps were echoing across the corridor. Kara was wielding the gun but thankfully she didn’t need to use it as that side of the ship looked more deserted, the room she had seen was only steps away.

    They managed to get through the door barely avoiding getting shot, Kara closed the door after her and took the glass case, deactivating the cloaking mode and shaking its contents till two small sun bombs dropped to the ground and illuminated the tight space. Lena was shielding her eyes at the corner, Kara had just put on her glasses to activate her suit as she could feel her powers coming back, the door whirred open revealing at least a half dozen Daxamites aiming at them. Kara rushed to shield Lena from the bullets that were literally raining down on them.

    Someone shouted as she heard behind the fog of artificial yellow sun. “Switch to Kryptonite!”

    In a millisecond she activated her suit’s anti-Kryptonite feature and raced across the room, pounding the Daxamites and flinging them across the corridor for them to stick to the nearest wall-like crushed flies. She could feel the ship had stopped to halt, probably because of the commotion she had created; as she raced back to gather Lena in her arms to carry her across the maze of corridors all the while knocking down every Daxamite on her way. She punched the ship’s interior walls a couple of times till the inside pressure dropped and the ship shook with an alarming force. Her next targets, the escape pods that were lined in the lower deck, using her superspeed she damaged the pods one by one so that there wouldn’t be anyone escaping the ship, especially Rhea. If the ship was going to sink, everyone was going to sink with it.

    Lena was trembling in her arms along the way, hugging tightly to her neck; Kara found the spacesuit she had located during their excursions at the ship and made Lena wear it quickly, it was a simple spacesuit but it would do the trick till the help came since the pressure was dropping in the chambers of the ship.

    Sirens continued to blare at an increasing level as the ship’s equilibrium was irrevocably disrupted, Daxamites were shooting randomly or running in chaos to the escape pods to find them broken.

    She and Lena were on the main deck, they could see a ship approaching their vessel at a full speed. Once it was close enough, Kara saw it was one of the Kryptonian ships, the one DEO was using with Alex, Sam, and J’onn on board.

    She didn’t get a scratch thanks to her sturdy suit but as the pressure was dropping and the ship’s gravity was exponentially decreasing she was having a hard time controlling her movements. And there were dozens of Daxamites piling up in the deck, most of them blindly raging on them, firing their guns which resulted in more holes in the ship’s exterior hence the faster sinking ship. Kara held onto Lena tightly, her spacesuit needed to be intact so that she could go to the approaching ship without any difficulty.

    “Lena, Lena, look at me? How are you feeling? Does it hurt too much?”

    Lena was panting in the space helmet, a patch of brownish dark hair was stuck on her forehead.

    “It-it hurts but it’s not unbearable. I can keep myself together till we leave this fucking ship. But-but Kara, don’t you need a suit too? Your supersuit is not compatible for zero-G, you need to-”

    Kara shook her head and saw DEO ship almost reaching to them, just seconds away till they were near the dock.

    “It’s okay, I can last long, don’t worry about me now, look, once they dock to this ship, I’m gonna open the gates and you are gonna go ahead, okay? You are gonna go straight ahead, Sam or Alex will take you and I’ll join you guys when I’m done here, I’m gonna try to get the antidote back if I can. But in any case, you are going first, and you are not waiting for me.”

    Lena clung to her supersuit, forcing Kara to meet her eyes. “What? No Kara, absolutely not, I’ll wait for you till you are done and we’ll go together-”

    Kara looked back, she could see Rhea coming towards them behind the group of Daxamite soldiers. The ship was aligning itself to the docking, once they were connected she had to send her away and let Alex or Sam get Lena to safety.

    “My love, there is no time, I promise I’ll come but I have to – I need to get rid of this ship first, you know my mother’s also coming so I’ll be okay but now you have to go Lena. You promised me you’d do what I say.”

    Kara snatched her hand back and opened the gate tying two ships to each other, she was trying to direct Lena to get inside the tunnel but Lena was trying to hold onto her hand.

    “Kara no-”

    “Lena, there is no time-”

    Just at that time, the other ship’s door hissed opened revealing a suit-clad Sam, heading towards their way with J’onn coming to stand next to her.

    “J’onn, what are you doing?!” Kara said looking baffled at the Martian. J’onn was in his full Martian form and he was carrying a small black box.

    “I’ve come to join you Supergirl, let’s do this.”

    “That is not the plan J’onn, I’m gonna stay here and you are supposed to help Alex-”

    “I’m staying here and making sure this ship’s destroyed while you are not getting killed in the process, understood?”

    Kara was about to retort back, Lena tried to drag her back to DEO’s ship with her but Sam came holding her arms over the suit.

    “Lena come on!” She shouted to Lena who was still holding Kara’s hand, she pulled Lena from her suit towards the back of their ship. Kara nodded her head towards Lena who was forcefully being dragged back to the ship.

    “Sam get her to Argo ASAP, Rhea injected something like Kryptonite, just tell them to be quick and careful-”

    “Okay I will, you be careful.” Sam shouted and closed the door after her, there were Daxamites at arm’s length but as Kara used her thunderclap, it propelled them backward.

    J’onn gave her the black box, while he started punching back the defenses Daxamite soldiers created, in the meantime Kara dived right into the soldiers, flying head on to Rhea. She barely managed to pick Rhea and thrust her backward along the ship’s long corridor but then Rhea revealed a Kryptonite knife to cut her, despite taking a couple of her blows her suit didn’t give off. They stumbled across the halls, trying to punch each other but it was a draw, Rhea was as powerful as Kara was and she wasn’t going to yield any time soon.

    Kara needed to distract her till she could plant the little box to the heart of the ship, to the ventilation system. They were slowly gravitating towards there as Rhea kicked Kara countless times, not giving her a chance to stand up, Kara was crawling on the ground till she saw her target.

    She had to endure a little bit more till she got to the ventilation room-

    Or maybe there could be a…shortcut.

    She threw the bag with enough force till it pierced through the gate of the ventilation room, and when she shot beams aiming the bag it exploded into a cloud of overwhelming dust quickly covering the space. Rhea was about to lunge at her but she froze mid-air, her nostrils flaring and her hands turning into concrete. She looked at her hands with panic, it was spreading fast.

    “What-what have you done?”

    Kara stood up as she swooped her by snatching her neck. “Gasified lead. Now, tell me where the antidote is, for the thing you injected Lena…”

    Rhea’s hands were frozen and her body was trembling as nearly half of her body had turned into stone, she was hysterically mumbling.

    “No no, this can’t be…my people, my son- I’m the queen of-”

    Kara shook her body till her manic eyes met with hers.

    “Rhea, tell me where the antidote is-”

    “There is no antidote. You are going to lose everything you love Kara Zor-El, I might not be here to see it but you’ll see, you are going to lose everything. You will witness everyone you love dying in front of you and-and…”

    Kara crushed her stiff hands till they evaporated into dust, making Rhea scream in agony.

    “If you will tell me where the antidote is, I will spare your son’s life, I won’t touch him Rhea, otherwise-”

    Rhea smiled between her tears, she looked absolutely terrifying. “My son will finish what I started, you’ll see Kara Zor-El. You will see.”

    Kara watched the deadly veins covering her face till her skin went gray-like, till all her body turned into a mass of stone first which piled into an air of dust under her hands when she tried to shake her one last time.

    She was standing on top of what was left of Rhea when J’onn came in a hurry.

    “Kara, Daxamite ships are coming to this way and your mother’s ship is here too, we need to leave now. Do you hear me? Kara?”

    Kara shook herself from the stupor, Rhea was gone. She was dead. Finally.

    But they needed to get rid of anyone who might be a threat, including Mon-El, Rhea’s seemingly dumb son. And all the Daxamites and their ships, even some of them were actually innocent or blindly following their leaders.

    “Let’s go.”



    Lena opened her eyes to a bright light hitting on her face, the last thing she remembered was passing out in Sam’s arms and then the darkness seeping her mind and then...

    She was furious, absolutely fuming at Kara who had left her even though she had promised not to leave her before, in any circumstance.

    How could she think sending her away while she stayed behind was acceptable? They had promised each other to fight anything coming to their way together, how could she break her promise so easily?

    She groaned with a sudden pain in her belly, when she tried to stand up a shadowy figure came running near her side.

    “Lena? Lena, how are you feeling?”

    Lena blinked her eyes, it was a bright space she had never seen before; it didn’t look like they were on a ship, it was a stranger place. When her eyes focused on the figure standing there, she noticed it was Sam, holding her hand tightly and looking at her with worried eyes.

    “Sam…Sam, where are we?”

    Sam looked around, there were some people in the room, some were looking at her blood pressure and some of them were looking at what seemed like big screens showing an ultrasound.

    The pain in her belly, Rhea’s injection before everything went to shit…

    “We are in Argo-”

    “My baby, Sam, is my baby okay? Rhea - she-injected me something looked like a Kryptonite-”

    Sam leaned down and held her hand with both of her hands. “Lena, it’s okay, calm down. The baby is fine, they cleaned that thing off your body.”

    Lena exhaled a forceful breath, her free hand went to her belly; in between leaving Kara behind, running away from Daxamites and going from ship to ship and to a new planet, she was so numb to worry about her baby. When Rhea jabbed that Kryptonite solution to her neck, first her whole body felt like on fire, and then came the crippling pain in her belly. She was so scared, not for herself but for their baby; the last couple of weeks were beyond intolerable and the thought of losing the baby was …

    “Where is Kara? And Alex?”

    Sam sighed standing up as went to look from ceiling-to-floor windows that offered a view of a futuristic-looking city Lena hadn’t noticed before. She averted her gaze to the sky, it was not blue as back on earth, there was a pale orange light casting a mellow hue over them; giving off the vibe that they were in another dimension.

    “They are still out there, transmission is weak when they are high in the orbit or that’s what they said.”

    Lena could pick up the distress in her voice, time was a lost concept to her at that point but them still fighting back meant trouble. That something was not right.

    “What about Rhea? What’s going on up there?”

    Sam trotted back and told something in Kryptonese, waited for the room to clear out as Kryptonians left the room hurriedly like they were keeping away, avoiding to look at them. Once they were alone, Sam looked at the door and offered a small smile.

    “I think they are afraid of me, I might have… caused a scene. They wanted me to leave the room when they were taking care of you and when I refused…I kinda threatened them. A bit. Okay, maybe it was too protective. But still.”

    Lena snorted and immediately regretted it as her abdomen ached with a slight movement.

    “So yeah, on another news… Rhea is…dead. Alex told me Kara and J’onn exploded her ship and got on another ship, that they are busy warding off incoming ships. They told me that there are dozens of ships coming this way, mostly mercenary ships hired by Rhea. Alex was with us when we landed here but then…As the number of ships coming increased, she had to leave. She took the DEO agents to join the forces of Queen Alura, she was calling to front lines, they made a barricade so nothing crosses it. I think it’ll take a while to-to pull through this thing. But Lena, don’t worry, I’m not going to leave you and everything will be fine once they are back, you’ll see.”

    Lena slumped down to the bed with a sigh.

    “I just want to go back to earth.”



    Kara burst through the doors, running towards where Sam was standing. She was covered in soot and her suit was torn off at some places, she looked absolutely washed out.

    “Sam-Sam, where is Lena? Is she okay? Is the baby okay?”

    Sam drew her in for a quick hug and patted her back, pointing the room with her head. There were also dark circles underneath her eyes and she was still wearing her suit except for the mask.

    Kara paced towards the room with haste, Alex was coming from behind with heavy steps. She also looked exhausted but there was a small smile on her face after seeing Sam. Sam smiled through her tears when she spotted Alex and bit her lips, opening her arms to welcome Alex.

    Alex quickened her pace and then when they hugged, their bodies clashed against each other, Sam squeezed her and scanned her body to see if anything was wrong.

    “Alex, baby, you okay? Uh, I was so worried when it took so long…”

    Alex was a bit shorter than her but when she pulled her in, Sam gladly complied as their lips met although briefly. Feeling all the fear and the worry washing away with the kiss, Sam sighed into the kiss. They stood there, hugging each other tightly till someone cleared their throat drawing their attention.

    J’onn was standing there with his arms open, looking equally exhausted.

    “Can I get a hug too?”


    Kara was panting when she reached the room and saw that Lena was fast asleep, her hand was laying protectively over her belly.

    The time she had spent up there fighting, it felt like a never-ending loop, stuck in the ship firing back all the while thinking if Lena was okay, their pup was okay…

    Now that finally they were done with Daxamites, their ships left as a fleet of wreckage, floating in the vastness of the space; she was able to finally return back to her wife, her family. She was so sick of drifting in the space, waking up to each day filled with challenges; she wanted peace. Getting far from all the trouble chasing them in the last month, disconnecting from everything; just to be with her wife and their pup, her little world.

    With her eyes watery she approached the bed and placed her hand over Lena’s, she couldn’t hear the heartbeat of their pup because of Argo’s synthetic red sun but she imagined the rhythmic ta-dum, beating peacefully. She would do anything for both of them if it meant slaughtering that many more of Daxamites.

    Then Lena opened her eyes and Kara’s heart faltered seeing those vulnerable eyes looking at her. She looked so small, Kara wanted to hug her tightly and not let go.

    “Kara?”

    She placed a light kiss on her temple. “I’m here baby.”

    Lena stood still, her eyes filled with unshed tears but she didn’t cry, instead, she stood up slowly with Kara’s help and then slapped her face hard.

    Kara felt her cheek burning with sudden impact as she held it, looking baffled like a drowning cow.

    “I-Lena-”

    “Don’t do something like that ever again, do you understand? Never. You promised me Kara, that we are going to stay together.”

    Kara opened her mouth to explain herself, to tell that what she did was essential for their safety, for their future. She hated leaving Lena alone and breaking her promises, even though it was for the greater good.

    They didn’t notice but Queen Alura was in the room, stood in the entrance watching them with apathetic eyes.

    “Inah, we have to talk.”

    Chapter 26
