    “Inah, we have to talk.”

    Well, Queen Alura certainly had a bossy attitude.

    Lena startled upon seeing the fierce-looking woman standing at the doorway with her hands tied behind her back. She was directly looking at Kara and anyone could see the simmering storm in her warm brown eyes. Ah, Lena could see clearly that Kara’s anger was directly inherited from her mother.

    Kara shifted beside her, her posture getting stiffer.

    “Can’t it wait mother, we just got here and I need to stay with my mate-”

    The woman didn’t even flinch and interjected. “I will be waiting for you in the main hall.”

    Kara sighed dejectedly and nodded, her hands finding their way to Lena’s to clasp them together which took the woman’s attention as her softened gaze landed on Lena and her big baby bump. Well, it took some time for the woman to acknowledge her presence.

    Her tone was nothing but formal. “Lena Luthor. I’ll be meeting with you with a proper introduction, but still, it’s good to see my daughter’s wife and the mother of my future grandchildren. I had hoped your first time on our planet wasn’t under these circumstances.”

    “It’s good to see you, too.” Lena managed to say out loud under the woman’s intense look, even though her tone or her words didn’t contain any open hostility, the way she called her Lena Luthor was enough to prove that she was maybe not too pleased with her presence.

    Queen nodded her head in Kara’s direction and left the room in long strides, finally leaving them alone. Kara turned to Lena and opened her mouth to say something but Lena was quicker.

    “It’s okay, go talk to her. But don’t let her crush you too hard, I need you back in one piece because I’m going to slap you a couple of times more too.” Lena said half-jokingly and patted Kara’s cheek, leaned back to the pillows. Kara let out a tired chuckle and placed a small kiss on her temple before leaving the room. Lena heard her voice just outside the door and barely seconds later, Alex came barreling down with Sam tailing her.

    “Lena? Are you alright- do you have any pain? Do you need anything? I can bring anything you want or-or we can call for someone to check up-Is the baby okay? Did something happen-”

    Lena chuckled to addled alpha and squeezed the hand Alex put on the bed.

    “I’m okay Alex, calm down, everything’s okay. Oh god Sam, if she can’t handle me getting a bit out of it, imagine how she would react when you are pregnant…”

    Alex’s face went through several exaggerated emotions but she turned to Sam in shock, even her ears were red.

    “S-Sam…Are-are you…”

    Sam rolled her eyes and slightly slapped Lena’s arm puffing out a laugh.

    “Don’t listen to her Alex, she’s just messing with us. And hey, we’ve already talked about waiting for a while, especially after the wedding-”

    Lena scrunched up her eyebrows and interjected Sam.

    “Umm, excuse me, what wedding are you talking about? What’s going on, guys?”

    Sam and Alex looked at each other and laughed at the baffled Luthor lying on the bed. Alex hopped in the bed and patted the place next to her for Sam to join in.

    “Oh well, we have some news for you.”


    Kara walked through the spacious halls and rooms with guards accompanying her till she reached her mother’s room or the main courtroom where she was sitting on her throne. It reminded her of the throne room in Krypton but this one was less grand, more minimal. Just like her mother.

    According to the deafening silence and the tenseness in the air, her mother was expecting Kara to join her impatiently. No matter what she had thought before about how this talk would go, Kara knew she would never be able to prepare herself fully for what’s coming next so she started slow:

    “Mother, let’s keep this short, I really need to take care of my wife, I haven’t seen her properly…”p>

    “How could you put your own people at risk like that? How could you endanger the very existence of our planet? Especially after all we’ve been through? How could you? How dare you, Kara Zor-El?”

    Although she wasn’t yelling, her words were cutting Kara like a knife as she tried to maintain her composure. The way she called her Kara Zor-El was like she was spitting her name out with immense displeasure. Kara tried to rein her impatience to get this talk over with, there was no need to fight with her mother now, especially now-

    “It’s not simple as that mother-”

    “Then explain it to me. Why it’s not simple as that.”

    Kara sighed dejected, her shoulder slumping with the exhaustion that was crushing her down. The last month she had enough things on her plate to test her limits at any capacity, she was emotionally drained and physically depleted. And she just wanted to be with her wife, to hold her in her arms; to alleviate the insurmountable worry she had experienced in the last 24 hours. But she needed to go through her mother first, and it was not an easy task.

    “Rhea had planted a Kryptonite bomb on earth, she was threatening us with it; so we - I had to make sure every Kryptonian on earth would be safe, that’s why we tried to come here in the first place. Then-then we thought it was over, then she abducted Lena-”

    Her mother cut in impatiently. “I’m aware of that part of the story. And I know you offered our location to Rhea willingly after she abducted your mate. I just can’t fathom, why would you do such a thing, how could you do such a thing. Was Argo that expendable in your eyes?”

    Kara’s voice rose as the anger flared up inside her. “How could I leave my mate in her hands? And my pup? What were you expecting me to do, mother? I had to offer her something she couldn’t say no, so I had to disclose Argo’s location.”

    “So, your mate of how many weeks in exchange for the lives of your people? One person for thousands of people?” Alura said icily, biting back. Her eyes were frosty and if Kara wasn’t so angry with her, she would consider calming down, not to challenge her mother’s alpha.

    “I suppose I can’t expect you to understand the bond I’m sharing with Lena. She is my everything, she is my family. Given how you treated father once-”

    The doors to the room opened and Imra Ardeen, the Titanian princess came sprinting to them.

    Oh it had been so long since the last Kara saw her.

    She was just like how Kara was remembering her, her soft hazel eyes, mahogany hair, the way her eyes shrank when she laughed…They knew each other since they were so little, they had grown up together. Imra was once her best friend, her confidant. And her assigned mate all those years ago. If Krypton hadn’t exploded, they were set to take the throne together, to unite their people.

    Her timing was perfect as if she hadn’t barged in exactly that moment, Kara would tell her mother some things she wouldn’t be able to take back, things she would later regret.

    Imra raced towards Kara and as soon as she saw her with open arms, she jumped to alpha’s neck.

    “Kara Zor-El. How long has it been? 10 years? 15 years?”

    Kara chuckled as she patted the omega’s back, leaving her onto the ground after spinning her for good measure.

    “Imra Ardeen. It’s so good to see you.”

    When they split, Imra noticed the tenseness in the room as the veins in Queen’s forehead were bulging and her face was red. Queen loved Imra, she saw her as her daughter; that’s why it was so easy for her to barge in like that without making Queen angry.

    “Sorry my Queen, was I interrupting something?” She asked while eyeing Kara whose expression changed into something stony just like her mother.

    Queen sighed and went to sit on her throne. “I was just asking how the future Queen of Argo could be so reckless? So inconsiderate of her own people? This is not how I raised my daughter. I would expect better than her.”

    “As I told you before, mother, I have no intention of being the Queen of Argo City. I don’t want it, I won’t do it. I’m so sorry that I had to put so many lives at risk but-but I had to. To save my family. And it was also the time that the existence of Argo was known among all aliens. You were hiding in shadows for years, whereas you could easily provide refugee to many aliens who have to live in hard conditions on earth-”

    “We didn’t come to this day by thinking like that, Inah. We survived because of my carefully thought policies. I had to build this place, bit by bit, in order to make it glorious like how Krypton was once, I had to work hard. I can’t expect you to understand the sacrifices I’ve made to build our nation-”

    Kara’s booming sound echoed in the room as both Alura and Imra recoiled.

    “Don’t talk to me about sacrifices mother! I have made enough sacrifices for the paths you have chosen for me. You and father. You chose to put me in that pod, you chose to leave me in the dark all those years…So I think I’m entitled to talk about this. And Krypton is gone, it’s gone to dust! There’s no way to bring it back now. Trying to cling to the old memories wouldn’t do any good, neither to yourself nor to our people. This is a new planet, a new chance to do things right; we can’t let our fear affect the way we live. How long were you thinking about staying hidden here? What about all those people living on earth with nowhere to go? You are talking like my rules, my way, my policies but what about your people? What do they want? Have you ever thought about getting their opinion on this, or-or anything? Considering our planet exploded because of scientists like my father, because of the greed of my family, I think these people put so much trust in you. In us.”

    Her mother's face went red to pale, Kara could see that she was seriously pissed, like she was gonna explode at any second. Imra stepped in again, just in time with a soothing voice.

    “Although I can’t support Kara’s choice of words, she has a point, my Queen. We’ve let our fear of Daxamites dictate our life for so long, we never considered those who need our help. There are thousands of aliens on earth, living in bad conditions. Maybe this is a good opportunity to extend a hand to them.”

    Queen Alura stood silent for seconds, her glare never leaving Kara’s face till it turned into a more neutral one.

    “Your concern is noted, Princess Kara. Maybe you can do something about it when you take over your place on this throne.” She said with a straight tone but Kara could see some cracks in her mother’s usually hard façade. Her rejection was finally getting through her, she just needed to see things from Kara’s perspective. She approached her mother carefully and took down on a knee in front of her.

    “Mother.”

    She waited till Queen Alura met with her eyes, then she extended her hand, putting it on top of hers. Alura In-Ze was not a woman showing motherly affection frequently, but she also had her moments when Kara was little. She used to listen to her daughter even though Kara was small, she always cared for her daughter’s opinions. She was the one who taught Kara how to defend herself when she was barely able to hold a spear. Their relationship had grown cold over the years they spent apart, especially after Kara learned all about the existence of Argo and that her mother was alive, she was so angry at her for keeping truths from her, so she had started to challenge her mother openly which left their already cold connection more frayed. But maybe it was time to be frank with each other to pair their mutual trust and understanding.

    And Kara was at fault too, regardless of whatever, she had endangered her people recklessly. Her mother was right about that part. And it certainly proved her point, that she could never be a competent leader for their people.

    “I understand that you want me to succeed you, to be the ruler you’ve always wanted me to be, but…I have never wanted it. Even when I was a little child, the responsibility of being Kara Zor-El was too heavy for me, I-I searched for a way to escape from that. To be the Kara you and father wanted me to be. So, when my pod landed on earth, I felt like I was given a second chance in life, I got to be the person I could be. Without any overbearing responsibilities or people’s expectations. Earth…it’s my home. It’s where I live now, where I want to spend the rest of my life, now that I have a mate and a pup on the way. I want…I want my children to live without feeling the obligation to do their best to honor their legacy. I just want a normal life for them. For myself too. Do you understand? This is why I can’t stay here and can’t do the things you want me to do. And you are right. You are right that I…I put our people at risk. But it was a choice I would do every time when I need to, my family comes first above everything. But…but I’m sorry. I’m sorry that I’ve let you down, that I’ve put Argo at risk.”

    Alura’s eyes softened as Kara talked, the shift in her stance was so palpable as the Queen’s sagged after a while and she let out a shaky exhale. She pursed her lips and closed her eyes, her hand was still underneath Kara’s; Kara expected her to draw her hand back and maybe scold her further for her foolish-

    “Is it…is what you told me really what you want?” She said with a small voice, not opening her eyes. It was the first time Kara was seeing the nuanced crinkles in her mother’s face, she looked old. Old enough to show the stress and the experience she had to go through; she was still a beautiful woman, a charming alpha but she looked…tired. Liked she needed a break. Maybe that was why she was pushing Kara to decide on accepting her place on the throne so hard lately, after all those years of fighting alone she definitely deserved a good retirement. Kara would be glad to see it, but she wouldn’t want to sacrifice her own happiness, her life in the name of that.

    “Yes, mother. This is what I want.” She said with resoluteness in her voice. Her mother looked at her for seconds that felt like years, then stood up suddenly, taking her hand.

    “Then, in the presence of Imra Ardeen of Titan; I, Alura In-Ze, the Queen of Argo City, release you from all the royal duties Kara Zor-El. You are no longer the heir to the Argo’s throne.”

    Kara stood up slowly, her height matching with her mother’s; she bowed her head for the last time and smiled at her mother who had tears in her eyes. She couldn’t help but hug her mother tightly, wrapping her arms around the woman’s form as Alura stilled. After a couple of seconds, her mother’s stiffness dissipated and she enveloped Kara in an equally tight hug.

    “I’ve missed holding you, my daughter. I’ve missed talking to you without fighting all the time.”

    Kara drew back with unshed tears in her eyes too, it had been too long holding her mother too close. She didn’t want their bond to be diminished; just like how she got on with Eliza, maybe she could be close with her mother too, after all. In time.

    But also, there was a pressing matter of who would take her mother’s place as Kara asked:

    “But mother, did you think about who would be taking your place after you abdicate?”

    Her mother sighed and nodded with a small smile. “Kal-El seemed eager in our last talk about returning back to Argo, after what’s happening on earth. Maybe I can guide him through everything, till he matures to rule by himself.”

    Kara nodded, Kal-El could be a good choice. Although he was a bit of a prick, he had always cared about the interests of aliens, that was how he got Kara into the Supergirl thing in the first place.

    “That’s a very good idea, mother.”

    Queen Alura wiped the tears that escaped her eyes and coughed up, changing her stance.

    “Alright then, now I can finally meet with your mate and your pup.”



    “Are you sure I cannot convince you to stay a bit longer?” Alura asked as they were headed to the ship deck; Alex, Sam, and J’onn were completing the preparations for the travel, Lena and Kara were slowly walking towards the ship with guards following them.


    It had been two days since Kara and the rest of the soldiers returned to Argo after they made sure that there were no Daxamites or any other hostile ships lurking around. There was still alertness among the Kryptonians but at least they were ready for what could come next, some small ships were touring around the orbit to detect if anything was wrong, the defense shields were put in place -which was a magnificent product of engineering according to Lena who had a chance to look through Kryptonian technology briefly, and with the continuation of silence and inactivity, they had decided to return back to earth, to their home.

    Lena had expected a colorful protest from Queen Alura -or just Alura as she was instructed to call her so, but after her talk with Kara the other day, they seemed to be on better terms. She wasn’t pushing Kara to stay anymore, the abdication of Kara from Argo’s line of succession had caused some stir among the court and some royals apparently, but it was final. She was no longer Princess Kara Zor-El, just Kara Zor-El. Or even better, Kara Danvers. Her mate and her wife.

    People of Argo were a bit distant and hesitant towards them at first, but after seeing Queen supporting them and welcoming them, they were much candid with their approach. Especially Lena was quite an attraction source with her baby bump, everyone cooing over her and curiously inspecting her. The time had flown by so quickly as the birth was getting close exponentially according to Argo’s medicians, she and Kara were eager to welcome their daughter. Finally, they were able to learn about the pup’s gender and it didn’t quite faze Kara, she was over the moon; whereas Lena only wanted their pup to be healthy. And she wanted to be back on earth before the birth, although Kryptonian technology could handle the delivery perfectly well, being on a foreign planet lightyears away from home was a bit stressful. So Lena was looking forward to getting on that ship and returning, even it meant spending a bit more time on space which had become traumatic for every one of them.


    Being on Argo was pleasant for them, especially for Sam; who was finally able to have some answers to her questions about her real parents. It was a shock for everyone to find out that Sam was actually related to Kara, through Kara’s father Zor-El. Zor-El had a brother called Jor-El who was the father to Kal-El, the Superman as the world knew him, but El brothers had another half-brother, Im-El according to what Argoan Kelex told them. Apparently, Im-El had traveled to earth, much earlier than any of Kryptonians and he had mated with a human omega there, Sam’s mother. They had left Sam with a trusted friend on earth to travel back to Krypton, only to witness its explosion. After that, it was the known story; Sam growing up without knowing her true parents and heritage till one day her powers manifested. It was a lot to take in at one go, the revelation had left everyone flabbergasted. Not only she was related to Kara, but Sam was also actually a Kryptonian royal; originally in line with the succession of Argo. Which was something not so appealing according to Sam who was very quick to dismiss any claim she could have over the throne after Kara’s abdication. Everyone had agreed that Kal-El was going to be a good king to Argo, so that was the end of any topic on succession.

    Kara was quick to tease Alex about the fact that she and Sam were cousins.

    “I guess you need my permission to marry Sam now, don’t you Alex? You know, as the head of House El-”

    The smack she got in return had brought laughter to anyone around, including the usually stoic Queen Alura.

    Lena was really glad to see Sam getting some closure, she was their rock, literally with everything she had done; she had even left Ruby back on earth to help her and Kara. It was the least they could do, to return to earth after spending so much time across space. She could be Kara’s cousin but she was a sister to Lena, one of the most important people in her life. Seeing Sam so happy with Alex beside her and now united with her remaining part of her biological family, it had brought tears to her eyes as Lena hugged Sam tightly and both of them had cried their eyes out. They were a family, even before they knew about Sam’s origin; a strong family not bounded by blood but love.


    And there was Imra Ardeen, Kara’s ex. Seeing her so beautiful and noble in her royal tunic was making Lena a bit jealous and protective of Kara, especially now that she knew Imra and Kara were meant to be mated if Kara had stayed in Krypton or Argo. They were meant to rule together, as the power couple; Imra was a well-respected figure among Kryptonians. So it was safe to say, meeting her was challenging for Lena, she was already so possessive of her alpha; and now that seeing an attractive omega parading around as Kara’s best friend was a boost for her to unleash some aggressive pheromones. Thank gods, Kara was very careful around Imra, not to do anything that would upset Lena as she had told about their history quite extensively before. Despite her jealous streak, Lena was awfully aware that the alpha’s eyes were not seeing anyone other than her, especially with the way she was adoring her. Still, it was a good reminder for Lena to thank the universe for crossing her path with Kara, having her in her life.


    With the promise of a visit from Queen Alura, they had set course back to earth. Everyone was looking exhausted but happy, now that all trouble was done and through. Even J’onn had excused himself after spending a couple of hours together, mumbling something about ‘spending his time in his room till they return to earth’. Well, he was not the only one; Sam and Alex had excused themselves, Kara and Lena knew better than to keep them from having a well-deserved rest. All of the crew had gone silent too after some time, everyone was busy with recharging. Kara was lying on their bed with her head on Lena’s lap, Lena was distractedly stroking her hair.

    “What are you thinking?” Kara asked slowly.

    “Nothing. Everything. I don’t know.”

    Kara straightened up, leaning on the headboard to pull Lena into her arms.

    “I’m sorry baby, I’m really sorry that you had to go through all of that. It was my fault-”

    Lena held Kara’s hands between hers and forced alpha to look into her eyes.

    “Nothing was your fault, do you hear me Kara? Nothing. It was a crazy couple of weeks, that’s all. But it was not your fault, you need to stop blaming yourself for everything.”

    Kara sighed as her beautiful blue eyes darkened.

    “I can’t stop thinking about…If I didn’t bring all this chaos into your life-”

    Lena sighed and offered a strained smile. Her eyes watered upon remembering what was waiting for her back on earth. She needed to arrange a funeral for her brother, the brother she had killed. She had a mother who wasn’t remembering her, who didn’t know anything about her. And another mother who hated her wife. A company that was quite possibly in shambles and a world leaning towards the mayhem day by day. Maybe it was absurd of them to want to go back to earth after all.

    “You didn’t bring chaos to my life Kara, I would want to say it was me who brought chaos into your life but…We are past the point of blaming ourselves. We have to be. We have so many things to do when we are back on earth, mistakes to fix. We just need to forget about this past month, and-and try to think everything’s gonna be okay.”

    Kara wiped off some tears falling down to her cheeks as Lena leaned her head, allowing alpha to kiss her temple. After everything that had happened, there was no guarantee of bad things wouldn’t happen but also, there was the possibility or the hope of the good things that could happen. Wasn’t that hope the thing driving everyone to go forward? Just a glimmer of hope, propelling the persistence of hanging on?


    They spent a luxuriously long time there, hugging each other tightly and talking things through. It was a similar routine they had cultivated on the Daxamite ship, but this time they were free of survival fear; much easier for Kara to fall asleep in Lena’s arms while Lena watched her sleeping. After losing the track of time, holed up in their room for a while; finally, they woke up famished and ready to venture outside of their room, a little exercise would probably do wonders for Lena’s swollen feet and Kara’s rigid muscles.

    Maybe it was good to trade their usual outfits with Kryptonian tunics as they were more loose and breathable; they had a matching set as a gift from Queen Alura. Kara was babbling about finding some food for them, maybe waffles or pancake which would be hard to find on a goddamn spaceship but still, there was no harm with trying.

    The corridors were pretty silent and isolated as Lena’s laughter echoed in the spacious area because of the face Kara made after she had told the food she missed the most was broccoli.

    They were walking hand in hand down the corridor when the alarms started to blare.

    Goddamn alarms.

    They were so loud as Lena burrowed her body to Kara’s who looked around and hugged her tightly. Kara pointed to a nearby window, there was a ship approaching them at full speed.

    “I need you to go to our room :i zhao, stay there till I take care of this, okay? And don’t open the door unless it’s me.”

    She waited for Lena’s approval but Lena wrapped her arms around Kara’s, detaining her from leaving.

    “But Kara, I-” She tried but Kara was adamant.

    “I promise you, everything’s gonna be okay.”

    The next second, there was an ominous booming sound heard across the ship and everything went black.

    Chapter 27

    Chapter Notes

    hello everyone, welcome to the final chapter of this story!

    before bidding a farewell to this story, gonna let you read it first; so enjoy☄️

    “Lor, how many times do I have to tell you? That you shouldn’t leave the Cat alone with Krypto?”

    Kara grunted in lieu of a reply, it was a funny coincidence that Andrea Rojas’ wife was pregnant at the same time with Lena although their pup’s birth was just around the corner whereas Lena had a little more than a month.

    Even though they had started off badly, Kara had grown to respect Andrea; she was sincere about her loyalty and friendship to Lena, and more importantly, she hadn’t tried to do something with Lena after Kara claimed her. At first, it was still hard to see her hanging around Lena like she was still an alpha stalking prey but seeing how she treated Lena out of respectable friendship, Kara had started to feel more relaxed towards her. Whenever they were sitting at a table together, there was still playful banter but that was all, everyone was respectful to everyone’s boundaries. And especially now that she was married to a lovely omega and they were expecting their first pup, they had more time to bond over as alphas, Kara and Andrea.


    Kara didn’t notice since she was lost in her thoughts that Lena had again fallen asleep in her lap, but when Lena’s cold little nose burrowed in her neck she chuckled lightly and lifted her wife in bridal style. Lillian had returned to the city to god knew what to do, but except for her everyone was going to stay over their place for the weekend, Alex and Sam were going to spend a bit more time with them. So Kara turned to everyone with Lena in her arms and bid them goodnight.

    She slowly went upstairs to their room, checked to see that Lena was still fast asleep. As they passed through Lori’s door, Kara noticed the door was half-open and Roisin was next to Lori on the bed like she was telling her a story. Kara placed Lena on the bed and changed her clothes, after wrapping her body to the duvet tightly, she went to check up on Lori.

    The galaxy-themed light was on, lightly illuminating the place -which was Lillian’s gift, Lori had fallen in love with it; Roisin was stroking her long blond hair and telling some story to the little girl.

    “So when someone falls in love, is it about the stars?” Lori asked with a sleepy voice, her eyes were half-closed. Roisin chuckled at her question and combed her hair.

    “Some say as so, yes.”

    “What do they say?”

    “You know, it’s a very special and rare event that sometimes stars position themselves in a special way; so when they align, people believe that something miraculous will happen. Two different paths meeting, two people uniting to become one, it is the most precious thing in the whole universe, the most miraculous thing. That’s why they say it all begins when stars align, we fall in love.”

    Kara listened to Roisin speaking softly to their daughter, Lori was seconds away from passing out as Roisin wrapped her up with a blanket and left the room to find Kara watching them.

    “Thank you for being here Roisin, it means a lot to Lena, to Lori, and me. Really, thank you.” She said slowly and sincerely, it was so good to have her. Roisin waved her head and held her hand.

    “No, I should be thanking you. I…I was scared before, and if it wasn’t for you convincing me, I wouldn’t have the courage to come up here and meet with my daughter and my granddaughter. So thank you Kara, thank you so much.”

    Kara saw the tears in the green orbs looking awfully similar to Lena’s and she drew the woman for a hug, after exchanging hushed sobs they parted ways and Kara returned back to their room only to find Lena sitting on their bed with tender eyes.

    “I’m sorry baby, did I wake you up? I was just checking-”

    Lena shook her head and gestured her to join. “Just strip down and come here.”

    Kara raced to shed her clothes till she was wearing her undershirt and boxers, she jumped to the bed next to Lena. Lena opened her arms as Kara happily dove into them, sighing a breath. Just when their breaths evened out, Lena mumbled against her temple.

    “I love you Kara.”

    “I love you too my wife, my everything.”
